市場焦點
關注新一輪通膨數據以評估利率路徑
聯準會
• 聯邦基準利率維持在 3.50%~3.75%，支持雙重使命 ，重申其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策 。
• 儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來高度不確定性 ，但經濟活動仍以穩健步伐擴張 ，生產力增長
與資本投資表現強勁。就業增長與勞動力人口保持同步，失業率則幾無變動。
• 相較於委員會 2%目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，反映部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊導致
價格上漲，委員會將實現價格穩定。
日本
• 日本央行於政策利率由0.75%上調至1%，是自1995年以來的最高水準，瑞穗銀行預估，若通
膨與經濟動能持穩 ，日本央行最快可望於第四季再升息至 1.25%。日本4月核心機械訂單大幅
優於市場預期 ，高附加價值製造鏈仍受全球科技投資循環支撐 。日股中長期表現值得留意 ，

產業配置上，可留意銀行、半導體設備、關鍵材料、被動元件以及其他政策受惠的類股。
新興債
• 自2025年以來，新興市場主權信用評等持續改善 ，淨升評次數創歷史新高 ，並延續至今年，
且新興債市面對今年美伊衝突與油價上漲衝擊亦展現其韌性 ，代表經過多年的體質改善與本
幣債比重的拉高 ，新興主權國家已發生結構性改變 ，持續關注評等分化與原油價格風險 ，投
資策略上，建議可分批布局原油出口國或非投資等級國家債券。

資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.6.18
貨幣決議與聲明稿差異
• 聯準會6月按兵不動，聯邦基準利率維持在3.50%~3.75%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為6月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的4月聲明措辭
近期指標表明，經濟活動以穩健的速度擴張。平均而言，就業增長仍然緩慢，失業率近幾個月變化不大。通膨處於高位部分原因在於近
期全球能源價格上漲。委員會力求長期內實現最大化就業和2%的通膨率。中東局勢的發展對經濟前景帶來高度不確定性。委員會密切
關注可能影響其雙重使命的風險因素。
FOMC以12票贊成，0票反對通過以下聲明:為支持自身目標，委員會決定，將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯
準會的雙重使命。委員會重申其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。在考慮對聯邦基金利率目標範圍進一步調整的幅度和時機方面，委員

會將仔細評估未來的資料、不斷變化的前景和風險平衡。委員會堅定承諾支持最大化就業 ，以及讓通膨回到其目標2%。在評估適當的
貨幣政策立場時，委員會將繼續監測最新資訊對經濟前景的影響。若出現可能阻礙實現目標的風險，委員會將準備好酌情調整貨幣政策
立場。委員會的評估將參考廣泛資訊，包括勞動力市場狀況、通膨壓力和通膨預期，以及金融和國際形勢變化的資料。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高度的不確定性，但經濟活動仍以穩健的步伐擴張。生產力增長與資本投資表現強勁。就業增長與勞
動力人口保持同步，失業率則幾無變動。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的價格上漲 。委員
會將實現價格穩定。

兆豐銀行所做任何投資意見與市場分析結果，係依據資料製作當時情況進行判斷

顧
景氣延續增長但美國製造業動能放緩，供應鏈壓力再度升高
• 景氣擴張但動能趨緩：紐約聯準銀行6月Empire State製造業景氣指數降至5.7，較5月明顯回落，顯示企業活動仍維持擴張，
但成長力道有所減弱 ，而新訂單與出貨小幅增加 ，未出貨訂單持續累積 ，反映終端需求仍具支撐 ，但供應鏈調整速度仍落
後於需求恢復。同時就業指數連續五個月成長，平均工時同步增加，顯示企業對未來需求仍具一定信心，持續擴充產能。
• 成本壓力與供應瓶頸仍待緩解： 支付價格指數維持在 61.0，售價指數亦高達31.4，顯示企業成本壓力仍持續向終端售價傳
導；供應鏈指數降至 -13.9，創2022年以來新低 ，交貨時間再度拉長 ，反映供應鏈緊張情況有所升溫 。儘管企業面臨成本
上升與供應受限挑戰 ，但在AI投資、企業資本支出及庫存回補需求支撐下 ，多數廠商仍預期未來六個月景氣維持擴張 ，惟

售價上漲壓力可能同步升高。
• 市場觀察：美國製造業仍受惠於 AI基礎建設投資、企業資本支出及庫存循環帶動 ，但供應鏈瓶頸與成本壓力可能壓縮企業
獲利空間。建議關注美國工業類股 、全球半導體設備供應鏈及科技創新 產業，同時搭配美國短天期債券 ，提高投資組合收
益穩定性；若供應鏈緊張情勢延續，可適度配置能源與天然資源相關資產，作為抗通膨與分散風險工具。
資料來源：New York Fed Empire State Manufacturing Survey ， 2026.6.16
Empire State製造業指數變化

顧
美國零售銷售優於預期，消費韌性支撐經濟溫和成長
• 消費動能穩健 ，內需持續支撐景氣： 美國5月零售銷售月增 0.9%，高於市場預期的 0.5%，核心控制組銷售同步成長
0.7%，顯示即使面對能源價格波動與高利率環境 ，勞動市場穩健 、所得成長及資產價格上揚仍支撐消費支出 ，家具、
汽車及電子商務銷售表現尤為突出，反映美國內需動能仍具韌性，消費者支出尚未出現明顯轉弱跡象。
• 經濟維持擴張，聯準會政策趨於謹慎： 5月零售銷售優於預期 ，加上近期就業與通膨數據整體仍具韌性 ，顯示經濟活動
維持溫和成長，使聯準會短期內傾向維持審慎觀望立場 。穩健的消費需求有助支撐企業獲利與經濟成長 ，但通膨回落趨
勢仍需進一步確認，後續利率政策路徑仍將取決於經濟成長、勞動市場與通膨數據表現。

• 後市觀察：美國經濟呈現「溫和成長、高利率延續」格局，消費韌性有助企業獲利維持成長，短期有利美國大型科技股、
數位消費及優質企業債表現；配置上建議以美國大型成長股及 AI受惠產業為核心，搭配中短天期投資級債券，在掌握企
業獲利成長機會的同時兼顧收益與波動管理。
美國零售銷售月增率趨勢

日本央行升息並於預計於明年暫停縮減購債
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.6.16 資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.6.16
即使日本央行暫停縮減日本國債購買規模，量化緊縮仍將持續日本央行政策委員會政策傾向
• 日本央行如預期升息1碼：日本央行於在6月15日—16日的會議上將政策利率由0.75%上調至1%，是自1995年以來的最高
水準，目的是為了抑制中東緊張局勢帶來的高油價導致通膨加劇的風險。瑞穗銀行預估，若通膨與經濟動能持穩，日本央
行最快可望於第四季再升息至1.25%。
• 預計於明年停止縮減購債：購債政策方面將按照現行計劃，在2027年1月至3月之前繼續每季度減少2,000億日元的購買規
模，並規劃於同年4月停止減少購債，將每月購債金額維持在約2兆日圓。日本長期利率曾一度快速上升，債券市場出現不

穩定局面的次數也有所增加。如果日本央行停止減少國債購買，預計將緩解市場對供需惡化的擔憂，並有助於穩定市場。

• 日本4月核心機械訂單月增8.7%，企業資本支出動能強：日本4月核心機械訂單月增8.7%，大幅優於市場預期的0.5%，年增
率達15.6%，同樣高於市場預期的8.7%，反映製造業與非製造業資本支出意願仍具韌性。AI、半導體設備、電子零組件、自
動化與節能投資需求，可能是帶動機械訂單回升的重要因素，也與同日公布的5月出口年增17%優於預期相互呼應，顯示日

進一步改善。在整體實質薪資增長趨勢不變，有助進一步支持內需與企業營收成長，推動經濟正向循環。日股中長期表現值
得留意，產業配置上，可留意銀行、半導體設備、關鍵材料、被動元件以及其他政策受惠的類股。

新興債投資機會與風險
• 新興債投資機會與風險： 自2025年以來，新興市場主權信用評等持續改善 ，淨升評次數創歷史新高 ，並延續至今年 ，其中
評級改善主要集中於CCC及以下國家，包括烏克蘭、玻利維亞、阿根廷與厄瓜多等，主因為財政體質改善，而BB等級為唯一
出現淨降評的區塊，值得注意的是倘若油價未來維持高位 ，將對原油進口國的財政與經常帳造成壓力 ，可能抑制信用升評空
間，因此相對看好原油出口國。
• 總結：今年以來新興債市面對美伊衝突與油價上漲衝擊 ，僅修正近3.5%且不到2個半月時間即修復，代表經過多年的體質改
善與本幣債比重的拉高，新興主權國家的韌性已發生結構性改變 ，投資上仍需關注評等分化與原油價格風險 ，新興債建議可
分批布局原油出口國或非投資等級國家債券，以掌握相對價值與風險調整後報酬。

2026至今新興市場主權信評表現佳

上週黃金價格震盪，央行購金意願創歷史新高
• 中東能源通膨壓力降溫，但聯準會升息預期升溫：上週先漲後跌，市場正在消化美伊和談消息與聯準會升息預期，黃金價格
在4,200美元附近震盪。不過由於聯準會大幅升息的擔憂有所收斂 ，花旗銀行上調3個月黃金目標價格至 4,500美元，6至12
個月目標價5,000美元；同時，巴克萊銀行表示年底金價上看4,800美元。
• 全球央行購金意願創新高： 世界黃金協會對全球 74家央行的調查中顯示 ，有45%的受訪央行計劃在未來一年增加黃金儲備 ，
為統計開始以來(2018年)的最高水平。同時，74%認為5年內美元在全球儲備占比將下降，持續反映央行多元儲備的趨勢。
• 觀點：黃金價格自今年1月底以來陷入修正區間 ，主要反映短線漲多回跌的投機部位調整 ，以及能源通膨造成實質利率上行

的壓力。由於高利率環境可能會比年初時預測的更久，黃金短期缺乏上行動能，不過在央行買盤支撐下，中期多頭的格局尚
未改變，建議投資人逢修正分批布局，以中長期持有的角度配置。
資料來源：Bloomberg、WGC，2026.6.16。

2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

■ 기아 26.2Q 매출액 32.4조원 (+10.4% YoY), 영업이익 2.7조원 (-2.7% YoY, 8.3% OPM) 전망. 기아 26.2Q
국내 도매 판매의 경우 154,254대 (+8.6% YoY)를 기록. 안전공업 화재 관련 생산 차질 영향이 소형 차량에
집중되어 있고 규모 또한 크지 않았음. 이외에 미국, 인도, 유럽 등 주요 지역에서도 준수한 판매 실적을 기록함에
따라 그룹사 대비 26.Q 호실적 전망. 단 기말 환율 상승 및 EV 판매 비중 확대에 따른 판매보증충당금 인식

증분이 2,000억원 내외 발생할 전망이고, 이란 사태에 따른 원자재 가격 상승 영향 또한 여전히 존재한다.
■ 26.2Q 미국 도매 판매 229,508대 (-1.2% YoY)를 기록. 그룹사 대비 미국 판매 성장 감소폭이 큰 모습이나,
우수한 인센티브 관리 역량이 이를 상쇄한다고 판단. 또한 26.2Q 미국 텔룰라이드 판매 대수는 전년 동기 대비
26.2% 증가한 37,674대를 기록했고, 특히 텔룰라이드 HEV 믹스는 53%에 달함. 26.2Q 유럽 도매 판매
153,356대 (+9.7% YoY)를 기록했고, 이는 중소형 EV를 중심으로 관찰되고 있는 산업 수요와 EV2 등의 신차
출시 효과가 더해진 결과. 단 기아도 유럽 시장의 경우에는 중국 업체와의 경쟁 심화에 따른 인센티브 부담 존재.
■ 기아의 경우 연초부터 시작된 그룹사 주가 상승 구간에서 소외됨. 상대적으로 우수한 본업 경쟁력에 근거한 동사

비중 확대 논리가 시장에 대두되었으나 크게 설득력을 가지지 못함. 이에 당사는 현대차그룹 차원의 미래 사업
성과가 구체화되는 26.2H부터 관련 낙수효과가 기아에게도 향할 것이라는 의견과 목표주가 240,000원 유지.

당보고서공표일기준으로해당기업과관련하여,
회사는해당종목을1%이상보유하고있지않습니다.
금융투자분석사와그배우자는해당기업의주식을보유하고있지않습니다.
당보고서는기관투자가및제3자에게E-mail등을통하여사전에배포된사실이없습니다.
회사는6개월간해당기업의유가증권발행과관련주관사로참여하지않았습니다.
당보고서에게재된내용들은본인의의견을정확하게반영하고있으며,외부의부당한압력이나간섭없이
작성되었음을확인합니다.
본분석자료는투자자의증권투자를돕기위한참고자료이며,따라서,본자료에의한투자자의투자결과에대해
어떠한목적의증빙자료로도사용될수없으며,어떠한경우에도작성자및당사의허가없이전재,복사또는
대여될수없습니다.무단전재등으로인한분쟁발생시법적책임이있음을주지하시기바랍니다.

## Document 179: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 두렵지 않다삼성전자(005930) 두렵지 않다두렵지 않다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 두렵지 않다삼성전자(005930) 두렵지 않다두렵지 않다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-06-25
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

기업분석 2026.06.25 www.daishin.com

Issue & News 
삼성전자  
(005930) 
 두렵지 않다 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 560,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.06.24) 
340,500 
반도체업종

 최근 주가 변동성이 심화되며, 때아닌 고점 논란이 제기. 
 흔들릴 필요가 없는 시점. 이익과 주주환원의 동반 개선을 기대 가능. 
 목표주가 560,000원과 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 두렵지 않다 
목표주가 560,000원과 매수의견 유지. 최근 주가 변동성이 심화되며, 때아닌 
고점 논란이 불거지고 있으나, 이에 흔들릴 필요가 없다고 생각. 
1. 견고해지는 이익의 성벽 
2Q26 실적은 성과급 충당금 인식 영향 (DS 사업부문: 10조 후반 추정)으로 
컨센서스를 하회할 것으로 전망 (영업이익 전망: 대신증권 82조원, 컨센서스 
86.8조원). 다만, 이는 단발성 이슈에 불과.  
AI 시장 선점을 위한 고객 전반의 설비투자 상향과 제한적 생산 증가 속, 반
도체 수급 괴리율은 지속 확대되고 있으며, 그 과정에서 메모리반도체 가격 
상승의 기울기는 보다 날카로워질 것. 2027년에는 공격적 판가 정상화 노력 
속 2H23-1H25에 경험했던 HBM 주도 DRAM ASP 성장이 재현될 수 있는 
환경 (2027년 HBM ASP: 전년대비 65% 상승 전망). 이를 반영하여, 2027년 
영업이익 전망치를 추가 상향 (기존: 503조원, 신규: 532조원). 
2. 자사주 매입과 주주환원 정책의 강화 
임직원 성과급 지급 목적의 자사주 매입 재개와 주주환원 정책의 강화 (특별
배당, 자사주 매입/소각 등) 등을 기대. 연내 창출될 FCF가 300조원 이상 
(M&A 차감 전)일 것으로 예상되는 만큼, 충분한 주주환원 외에도 DX 사업부
문의 미래 성장동력 확보를 위한 공격적 M&A 또한 추진 가능한 환경.  
3. 베일에 가려진 기회 
시장에서는 메모리반도체 외 사업부문의 경쟁력 상실을 우려. DX 사업부문의 
경우, 부품 가격 인상 및 경쟁 심화에 따른 하반기 손익 악화를, 비메모리반
도체의 경우, 성과급 충당금 확대에 따른 적자 기간의 장기화를 우려. 
시장의 우려는 합리적이라 생각. 다만, 기술 경쟁력 회복 구간에서 창출 가능
한 반도체 사업부문의 시너지 효과에도 주목할 필요가 있다는 판단.  
- 삼성전자의 주가가 가장 빛나는 구간은 종합 반도체 기업으로의 가치가 부
각될 때. 해당 가치가 부각되기 위해선 파운드리에서의 추가 수주가 필요. 
- 당사의 경우, 해당 가능성에 있어 긍정적. 제반 여건 (최선단공정 수율 안
정화, 고객 수요 증가율을 하회하는 파운드리 업계의 Capa 증가율 등)은 
Upcycle에서의 시너지 효과를 내기에 최적화된 국면에 위치.  
- 모바일 AP 중심의 사업 구조를 넘어, HBM Base Die, Auto, 모바일 AP, 
ASIC 등으로 대응 영역을 확대해갈 것으로 전망.  
- 단기 손익은 성과급 충당금 영향으로 적자가 불가피하나, 내재된 사업 경
쟁력의 개선 가치를 감안 시, Re-Rating의 여력은 충분하다는 판단. 현재와 
같은 저평가 구간에선 해당 가치에 대한 Pricing이 탄력적 주가 상승을 이끌 
수 있다는 점을 상기할 필요.

KOSPI 8471.02

## Document 184: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 중기 주가 상승세 유지 예상삼성전자(005930) 중기 주가 상승세 유지 예상중기 주가 상승세 유지 예상 - 기업

PER(배) 18.3 9.1 7.7 9.9
PBR(배) 1.9 3.5 2.4 2.0
ROE(%) 10.8 48.4 37.3 22.1
배당수익률(%) 1.4 0.5 0.5 0.5
EV/EBITDA(배) 7.5 5.0 3.8 4.1
주: K-IFRS연결요약재무제표
[투자포인트]
■ 직원들에 대한 신규 보상 체계를 감안한 동사 FY26 영업이익은 340조원으로 YoY 680% 증가할 전망이며 이에
기반한 FY26 예상 BPS는 10만원, ROE는 47%로 추정
■ 동사 FY26 예상 ROE는 지난 30년간 최고점이었던 FY00의 41%를 상회하므로, 동사 주가에 FY00 당시 동사
고점 P/B 배수 4.2배를 넘는 4.8배 적용 가능. 이를 감안해 P/B 4.8배를 FY26 BPS에 적용 시 48만원의 주가

도출. CY27의 메모리 가격 상승 지속 여부에 따라 동사 주가가 48만원 이상에서 추가 상승할 수 있을지가
결정될 것으로 판단
■ 1Q26, 2Q26의 반도체 부문 직원들에 대한 상여금 지급용 충당금이 한꺼번에 반영될 2Q26의 동사 영업이익은
76.1조원으로 예상됨. 2Q26 반도체 부문에서는 74.3조원의 영업이익이 발생할 전망이며 충당금이 없었다면
87.8조원에 달했을 것으로 추정됨. 반면 SDC, MX/NW 부문의 영업이익은 메모리 가격 급등에 의한 부작용에
따라 0.2조원, 1.0조원으로 축소될 전망
■ 메모리 반도체 업황은 적어도 CY27까지는 호황이 이어질 것으로 판단됨. CY27에는 DRAM 업계 생산 증가율이
올해의 25%에서 20% 이하로 하락하고, 빅테크 업체들이 Cash 부족에도 불구하고 유상 증자, SPV & JV 등의
방식으로 데이터센터 투자를 강하게 지속할 전망이기 때문

■ 최근 급등에 따른 단기 주가 조정이 나타날 수 있으나, 업황 및 실적 호조가 지속되고 글로벌 유동성 증감률의
상승이 2H26에 재개될 전망이므로 동사 주가의 중기 상승세는 유효할 것으로 판단됨

당보고서공표일기준으로해당기업과관련하여,
회사는해당종목을1%이상보유하고있지않습니다.
금융투자분석사와그배우자는해당기업의주식을보유하고있지않습니다.
당보고서는기관투자가및제3자에게E-mail등을통하여사전에배포된사실이없습니다.
회사는6개월간해당기업의유가증권발행과관련주관사로참여하지않았습니다.
당보고서에게재된내용들은본인의의견을정확하게반영하고있으며,외부의부당한압력이나간섭없이
작성되었음을확인합니다.
본분석자료는투자자의증권투자를돕기위한참고자료이며,따라서,본자료에의한투자자의투자결과에대해
어떠한목적의증빙자료로도사용될수없으며,어떠한경우에도작성자및당사의허가없이전재,복사또는
대여될수없습니다.무단전재등으로인한분쟁발생시법적책임이있음을주지하시기바랍니다.

## Document 190: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다LG에너지솔루션(373220) 그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다LG에너지솔루션(373220) 그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-07-08
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.07.08

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

그래도 꿈과 희망이 있다 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 470,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 332,000원(7/7)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 목표주가를 470,000원으로 
15% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 북미 EV 파우치 가동률 부진과 
ESS 신규 공장 초기 램프업 비용을 반영해 ‘26년 영업이익을 1조원에서 
6,300억원으로 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 그러나 유럽 전기차 판매량이 증
가세를 지속하고 있고 3Q26부터 GM 공장 가동 재개로 북미 EV 물량도 
점진적으로 회복될 전망이다. 동사는 Tesla와 43억달러 규모의 LFP 배터리 
공급계약을 확보했고, 한화큐셀과도 5GWh 규모의 ESS 배터리 공급계약을 
체결해 2024년 4.8GWh 계약에 이어 추가 물량을 확보했다. 이외에도 최
근 DTE에너지와 6GWh, 16억달러 규모의 ESS 공급계약을 체결하는 등 북
미 ESS 수주 모멘텀이 AI 데이터센터와 대형 전력망 프로젝트로 확대되고 
있다. 북미 ESS 생산능력도 ‘26년말 50GWh+로 확대될 예정이며, EV 수
요 부진으로 유휴화된 생산거점을 ESS로 전환해 신규 투자 부담을 낮추면
서 빠르게 캐파를 확보하고 있다. 단기 실적 부진은 지속되고 있으나 유럽 
EV 수요 회복, GM 공장 가동 재개, 46시리즈 출하 증가와 북미 ESS 가동 
본격화를 고려하면 실적과 주가의 바닥은 지나고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [2Q26 Review] 매출액 7.5조원(+15%qoq, +25%yoy), 영업이익 1,133
억원(흑전qoq, -77%yoy)으로 당사 추정을 하회하는 부진한 실적을 기록했
다. AMPC는 북미 ESS 생산량 증가로 2,410억원(+27%qoq)이 반영되었
으며, 이를 제외한 영업이익은 -1,277억원으로 3개 분기 연속 영업손실을 
기록했다. 1) 원통형 전지는 2170 및 46시리즈 물량이 동반 증가하며 가동
률이 70%를 상회해 매출액 2.2조원, 영업이익 1,673억원(영업이익률 
+7.5%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다(KITA 원통형 마진 스프레드 36달러
/kWh로 전분기 대비 5달러/kWh 개선). 2) EV 파우치는 유럽향 물량 회복
에도 GM 공장 가동 중단 영향으로 매출액 3.4조원, 영업손실 797억원(영
업이익률 -2.4%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. 관세 환급 반영에도 마진 스프
레드는 20달러/kWh로 여전히 고정비가 커버되지 않는 상황이다. 3) ESS는 
매출액 2.0조원, 영업이익 260억원(영업이익률 +1.3%)으로 추정한다. 신
규 공장 가동과 북미 출하량 증가로 외형 성장을 지속했으며, 2Q AMPC 증
분의 대부분을 차지한 것으로 보인다. AMPC 포함 시 흑자를 기록했으나 초
기 램프업 비용과 팩 조립 병목 영향으로 이를 제외한 수익성은 부진했다. 
 [3Q26 Preview] 매출액 7.9조원(+4%qoq, +39%yoy), 영업이익 2,113
억원(+87%qoq, -65%yoy)로 전망한다. 3Q부터 GM 공장 가동이 재개되며 
1~2Q 부진했던 북미 EV 파우치 물량이 회복되고, 유럽향 물량도 점진적으
로 증가할 전망이다. ESS는 초기 램프업 비용과 팩 조립 병목이 완전히 해
소되지 않아 3Q에도 AMPC를 제외한 흑자 전환은 어려울 것으로 예상된
다. ESS의 본격적인 수익성 턴어라운드 시점은 4Q26으로 예상된다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

1WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUSTRALIA &
NEW ZEALAND
WEEKLY
Week beginning 6 July 2026
In this week’s edition:
Economic Insight: China’s greatest opportunity and biggest threat.
The Week That Was: On the minds of policy makers.
Focus on New Zealand: Keep calm and focus on the data.

For the week ahead:
Australia: RBA Assistant Governor Hunter speaking.
New Zealand: RBNZ policy decision, Matariki public holiday.
Japan: Household spending, current account balance.
China: CPI, PPI, new lending data.
Euro Area: Retail sales, PPI, Sentix investor confidence.
United States: FOMC minutes, ISM services PMI, existing home sales, initial jobless claims.
Analysis and forecasts for this week’s key releases.
Information contained in this report current as at 3 July 2026.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• The first half of 2026 has highlighted China’s industrial
strength. Ahead, China’s greatest opportunity is also its
biggest threat – a recovery in the Chinese consumer.
• If authorities take the initiative and reset the consumer
story, GDP growth at or above 4.5% can be sustained, even

as the impetus from trade fades.
• Successfully holding Chinese GDP growth around 4.5%
through 2028 should allow the Renminbi to sustainably
appreciate back to 2022 and 2018’s, then short-lived, highs
against the US dollar around USD6.30.
• The implications for Australia and our dollar are difficult
to discern at this juncture. China continues to expand its
sourcing network across Asia, Africa and Latin America.
Australia is therefore unlikely to receive a material,

sustained dividend from increased commodity volumes.
• However, if we lean into Asia’s development, our
productivity and income prospects will grow, and our
dollar strengthen.
The first half of 2026 has highlighted China’s industrial
strength, the trade surplus widening back to near record
highs and investment in high-tech manufacturing and services
continuing at pace while authorities’ strategic reserves and
pro-active decision making offered robust protection against an

extended loss of oil supply. Ahead, China’s greatest opportunity
is also its biggest threat – a recovery in the Chinese consumer.
Before we delve into the detail of the consumer outlook, it is
important to dig deeper into recent industrial developments
and their implications. Having narrowed sharply in March to
USD51bn, China’s monthly trade surplus snapped back to
USD85bn in April and US105bn in May. The average for the
year-to-date is now USD91bn, just inside 2025’s USD99bn

and roughly triple 2019’s USD35bn. May’s 19% annual export
growth is arguably outsized, but high single-digit gains are
possible, if not probable, on a recurring basis. Note that it isn’t just
export demand that is buoying the surplus. Chinese production
is also increasingly outcompeting imports when it comes to
domestic demand, motor vehicles being a prime example. This is
also contributing to the widening trade surplus year-on-year.
Total fixed asset investment has disappointed of late, year-

to-date growth contracting 4.1% in May. While a loss of
momentum in utilities and transport and mixed results within
manufacturing allowed the headline contraction to occur, it
was education, health and property that drove the decline.
China’s greatest opportunity and biggest threat
Elliot Clarke
Head of International Economics,
Westpac Group
With respect to trade income generation, it is important to
recognise that investment has only plateaued after years of rapid

growth. Each year that new investment holds at this level, a wave
of additional capacity and efficiency is unleashed. Strengthening
relationships with neighbouring nations also hold considerable
promise. Indonesia and Vietnam are prime examples, having
youthful and driven populations of circa 300mn and 100mn and
Governments open to large-scale foreign investment.
The compounding income returns China has received through
trade post pandemic should have resulted in a strong and

confident household sector with burgeoning wealth. Yet,
highlighted by annual nominal retail sales growth of -0.6%yr
and entrenched weakness in property, this is not the case. In
part this is due to the hyper-competitive behaviour of Chinese
firms which has suppressed profitability – an issue authorities
are now seeking to redress through their anti-involution
initiatives. But the primary cause of consumer hardship is instead
the disconnect that has emerged between aggregate household

income and the growth of Chinese industry. If consumers are to
find their feet, the benefits of trade must pass through.
We expect this to occur but as a multi-stage, likely multi-year,
structural process beginning with pro-active stimulus later
this year. Initial steps are likely to focus on additional support
for the housing sector and renewed subsidies for discretionary
consumption. These steps are critical not only to incentivise
current demand but also to promote employment in related

sectors. Hiring by high-tech manufacturing and service firms
is, in contrast, unlikely to accelerate given these sectors’
long-standing preferences for capital over labour as well as
the efficiency benefits of automation and AI. To receive direct
benefits from China’s industrial development, the average
household will arguably need to wait until the equity valuations
of these titans of industry reflect their future promise.
Households will also need to have the confidence to put their

wealth ‘at risk’. Authorities could accelerate the development of
this linkage via incentives to directly invest in financial securities
and/or by building trust in wealth and retirement solutions, but
we suspect this will not be an immediate priority.
“the primary cause of consumer
hardship is instead the disconnect
that has emerged between
aggregate household income and
the growth of Chinese industry. "

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
If authorities take the initiative in coming months and reset
the consumer story, GDP growth can be sustained at or
above 4.5%, even as the impetus from trade fades. But, if the
Government only guards against the downside, growth is likely
to slow to 4.0% and become increasingly fragile.

With respect to currency markets, success with the consumer
through 2028 should be enough for the Renminbi to
sustainably appreciate back to 2022 and 2018’s, then short-
lived, highs against the US dollar around USD6.30. Breaking
through that level is entirely possible, albeit more likely outside
our current forecast window once the Renminbi’s growing share
of global trade and financial flows are more widely accepted.
For China’s competitiveness and the long-term trajectory of

the currency, it is important to emphasise that the nation’s
economic and financial development are not occurring in a
vacuum but rather in concert with the rest of Asia. On a trade-
weighted basis, the anticipated currency gains will be limited
and most likely offset by continued productivity wins.
The implications for Australia and our dollar are difficult to
discern at this juncture. China continues to expand not only
its production chain but also its sourcing network across

Asia, Africa and Latin America. As such, while commodity
prices should remain supportive, Australia is unlikely to
receive a material, lasting dividend from increased commodity
export volumes. Very clearly though, not only in China but
across Asia, a broad range of new opportunities are opening
up. If, as a nation, we lean into this economic and financial
development, our productivity and income prospects will
grow, benefitting the dollar. For now at least, the market is

likely to take a wait-and-see approach with respect to our
prospects, the Australian dollar lagging the performance of
the Renminbi and potentially other nations across Asia who
are poised to outperform.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: on the minds of policy makers
June and averaging a loss of 195k jobs per month over the past
three. Further, continuing the trend of the past 18 months, the
participation rate fell to 61.5% in June; had participation held

steady since January 2025, the unemployment rate would now
be above 5%, not the reported 4.2%. Taken together, these
outcomes imply the labour market is most likely marking time.
That said, the downside risks the household survey allude to
are worth close attention.
Also consistent with the FOMC becoming less concerned with
inflation in coming months was the latest ISM manufacturing
report. Most notably, the prices component fell 9.1pts to
73, signalling a turn in upstream price pressures. Together

with the decline in oil benchmarks in recent weeks and the
available detail of both the CPI and PPI, which have offered
little evidence of material secondary inflation effects from
the Middle East conflict, this moderation should alleviate
concerns amongst the FOMC that a sustained re-acceleration
in consumer inflation is a probable risk.
The preliminary release for June Euro Area inflation was also
supportive of an increasingly benign inflation outlook, prices

falling 0.1% versus a 0.1% expected gain. The improvement
was broad based too, annual headline inflation moderating
to 2.8%yr from 3.2%yr and core inflation to 2.4%yr from
2.6%yr. Importantly, this was achieved while the labour market
remains historically tight.
FOMC Chair Kevin Warsh picked up on this improvement
in inflation prospects at the ECB’s Sintra Forum on Central
Banking, highlighting that inflation expectations and risks
"have come down" in recent weeks. Admittedly he is focused

on the US but, per the Euro Area data above, the narrative
is broadly true for developed economies across the northern
hemisphere. The keynote panel at the conference also
discussed several other important topics including the need for
greater flexibility and timeliness when conducting monetary
policy, how AI advances are being factored into decision
making and the importance of safeguarding financial stability.
Finally to Asia where the Q2 Tankan was received positively,

participants focusing on the increase in large manufacturer
sentiment from 17 to 22. Other outcomes were less positive,
however. Smaller manufacturers are more cautious, as are
service firms. R&D expectations were also revised down, and
profitability and labour market indicators came under pressure.
Overall, the survey suggests the BoJ needs to keep evolving
risks in mind as they continue to normalise policy.
This week’s RBA minutes provided more colour around

the Monetary Policy Board’s (MPB) June deliberations.
Overall, the MPB views the economy as “operating with
excess demand and widespread inflationary pressures”.
On the domestic front, there were “somewhat differing
views” among MPB members regarding the extent of
current capacity pressures, but they are nonetheless seen as
elevated and a risk to the normalisation of inflation towards
target. On the Middle East conflict, the MPB welcomed
progress towards a resolution but still viewed the balance

of risks as firmly skewed to the upside for inflation and the
downside for growth. As such, “increasing the cash rate
target” will be considered if required.
Last week’s speech from Deputy Governor Hauser  shed
more light on the RBA’s thinking on the relationship between
inflation and unemployment. The main takeaway is that
inflation tends to be more sensitive when the economy is
already tight; but equally, that the policy trade-off in such
a situation allows more scope to focus on bringing inflation

down without a large cost to employment. These judgements
underscore the RBA’s successive rate hikes earlier in the year
and their subsequent decision to pause and assess in June.
The RBA is also closely monitoring trends in the housing
market and the implications for wealth and consumption.
Cotality’s latest home value index fell 0.4% in June, Sydney
and Melbourne’s correction deepening as price growth slowed
from a higher base across the smaller capitals. Together with

signs of slowing credit growth , evidence that the RBA’s rate
hikes and sentiment are weighing on the market is mounting.
Westpac is forecasting further house price declines through
the remainder of the year, though strong population growth
and tight supply will limit their scale.
Before moving offshore, a final note on trade. The goods
trade balance surprised materially to the downside in May,
flipping from a surplus of $1.4bn to a deficit of $3.0bn, the

largest in over a decade. Volatility in gold flows was one of
the chief culprits, accentuated by a surprisingly large fall
in iron ore exports and a solid lift in car imports (i.e. EVs).
Imports of equipment related to the data centre build-out have
moderated but are still well above historical norms. Expect
continued volatility over the coming year(s).
Over in the US, nonfarm payrolls disappointed in June, with
only 57k jobs created and April/May revised down by a

combined 74k. The three-month average is now 111k versus
164k in May, though June’s result still suggests labour demand
is at least keeping pace with supply. Household survey
employment is materially weaker, however, declining 507k in

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Keep calm and focus on the data
We expect the Reserve Bank of New Zealand (RBNZ) will
leave the Official Cash Rate (OCR) unchanged at 2.25%
at its 8 July policy meeting , while maintaining a data-
dependent tightening bias. The Monetary Policy Committee

(MPC) is likely to emphasise that future policy adjustments
will depend on evidence of second-round inflation
pressures, sustained wage growth, and rising medium- to
long-term inflation expectations. In addition, signs that the
economic recovery is gaining traction and that the output
gap is narrowing will also be important considerations in
determining when policy tightening becomes appropriate.
The RBNZ’s communications are expected to retain language

indicating that OCR increases are likely at upcoming meetings.
However, this guidance will increasingly be framed as
conditional, rather than indicative of a predetermined path.
This shift is important, as it reflects the MPC’s intention to re-
anchor expectations around the flow of data, particularly after
markets became overly focused on the prospect of a July rate
hike following the May Monetary Policy Statement (MPS).
While it is notable that three MPC members voted in favour of

a rate hike in May, developments over the past six weeks have
significantly altered the economic backdrop. In particular, the
easing of tensions in the Middle East and the associated sharp
decline in oil prices have materially reduced inflation risks. As
a result, it is entirely plausible that a formal vote on raising the
OCR may not be required at the July meeting.
If a vote does take place, we expect only a small minority to
support an immediate increase. It is unlikely that members who

favoured holding rates in May will shift towards tightening
at this stage. In addition, those who previously supported a
hike may instead prefer to wait for the upcoming flow of key
economic data before revisiting the case for higher interest
rates at the September meeting.
A key objective for the RBNZ’s communications at this meeting
will be to redirect market attention towards upcoming data
releases as the key determinants of future OCR decisions. The

MPC is likely to use this opportunity to correct the perception
that a July hike was effectively “pre-committed.” Instead, the
Bank will reaffirm that its forward guidance is conditional and
responsive to evolving economic conditions.
Looking further ahead, we expect the RBNZ to continue
signalling a path towards eventual policy normalisation, but
with a recalibrated timeline. Prior to the war in the Middle
East, the MPC’s baseline view, as articulated in their February

Statement, was for a single OCR increase no earlier than the
end of 2026. Given that inflation remains above previous
forecasts, the Bank may now indicate that the first increase
could occur as early as September. However, this will likely be
presented as a possibility rather than a certainty, with a clear
emphasis on the need for supporting evidence.
The trajectory of tightening beyond the initial move is also
expected to be gradual, particularly if second-round inflation

pressures remain contained. And on that front, this week’s
business confidence survey showed that the number of
businesses who are planning on raising prices has been
dropping back again as oil prices have eased. This week’s
consumer confidence survey similarly showed that inflation
expectations have dropped back to pre-Iran war levels.
Evidence on persistent second round pricing pressures from oil
prices now looks very thin indeed.
While consecutive rate increases from September cannot

be ruled out, this scenario is unlikely to be presented as the
central case. A slower pace of tightening would be more
consistent with the gradual recovery in activity and less-
worrying inflation outlook. Indeed, if incoming data point to
weaker growth or if the detail of the June quarter inflation
report (out on 21 July) is softer than expected, the start of the
tightening cycle could be pushed back to December.
In terms of financial markets pricing, we anticipate some

moderation in expectations for policy tightening over the
remainder of 2026. Pricing is likely to converge towards one
to two 25 basis point increases by year-end, with further
adjustments contingent on the evolving data flow.
Arguments in favour of a July hike
Arguments in favour of an immediate rate hike have centred
largely on interpretations of the May MPS as signalling a July
move. However, we think that is a misinterpretation of what
the RBNZ’s projections intended to signal. While the RBNZ’s

May Statement did indicate that the OCR was expected to
rise over the coming months, that was not a precommitment.
The projected path for the OCR is always dependent on the
evolution of economic conditions. Indeed, the Governor’s
remarks at the time deliberately emphasised uncertainty,
noting that rate increases would depend on factors such as
oil prices, the strength of growth, and evidence of persistent
inflation pressures. Similarly, comments from Assistant

Governor Silk have reinforced the message that all policy
options remain on the table heading into the July review.
Westpac New Zealand
Economics team

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Other arguments for tightening include the recent depreciation
of the New Zealand dollar, driven in part by widening interest
rate differentials with other economies. While a weaker
currency does add to inflation pressures, its persistence
remains uncertain. Moreover, currency depreciation also

supports the tradable sector and contributes to a rebalancing
of the economy. As such, exchange rate movements alone are
unlikely to justify an immediate policy response.
On the growth side, the easing of tensions in the Middle East
is expected to provide some support to activity in the second
half of 2026. Lower energy prices have improved the terms of
trade and have already underpinned modest gains in business
and consumer confidence. However, these improvements are

unlikely to fully restore the more optimistic growth outlook
presented in the February MPS. Survey evidence continues to
point to weak momentum, with backward-looking indicators
suggesting that the recovery remains in its early stages.
Arguments in favour of no change
In contrast, the case for holding the OCR steady is supported
by several factors. Most notably, the sharp decline in oil prices
(down around $40 /barrel since May) has significantly altered

the inflation outlook. Oil prices are now well below the levels
assumed in the RBNZ’s May projections, and forecasts for
inflation have been revised lower. We now expect CPI inflation
will peak at 4% in the June quarter before slowing to 3.5% by
the end of this year. That would be well below the 4.1% rate the
RBNZ assumed for year-end inflation in its May MPS projections.
Furthermore, with lower oil prices and near-term inflation, the
risk of higher fuel costs passing through to a broader uplift in

inflation pressures is also lower. That risk was a key concern
highlighted by MPC members who voted for a hike in May.
Measures of shorter-term inflation expectations have eased
as energy prices have declined. Similarly, the labour market
remains relatively soft, limiting the risk of wage-driven inflation.
Crucially, a substantial amount of new data will become
available between the July and September meetings. This
includes the June quarter CPI, business surveys, labour market

indicators, and key measures of inflation expectations. With the
sharp decline in energy prices significantly reducing the merit
of pre-emptive action, we suspect even more hawkish MPC
members will now be content to see what this data reveals.
Economic activity also appears to have been subdued in the
June quarter, with most indicators pointing to little or no
growth. This suggests that the output gap may have widened,
reinforcing the case for patience.

Globally, expectations for further interest rate increases have
also moderated, with many central banks adopting a more
cautious stance. This international backdrop supports a more
measured approach by the RBNZ.
Scenarios
The most likely outcome remains a balanced approach that
retains a tightening bias while underscoring data dependence.
However, more hawkish and dovish possibilities exist including:
• A hawkish scenario where the OCR is increased and language

suggesting the RBNZs views haven’t shifted much. In such
circumstances, markets would assume at least 3, possibly 4
hikes in aggregate for 2026. We see this as implausible and
assign a 10% probability to it.
• A dovish scenario that seriously questions a hike before
December. The forward guidance could be omitted noting
that inflation risks seem much less prominent relative to
the still large level of excess capacity. The press statement
could note that evidence of strong core inflation and

broadening second-round inflation pressures would need to
be seen to justify a lift in the OCR, and that such evidence is
lacking at this stage. This is more plausible and we attach a
30% probability to this scenario.

NZ: RBNZ Monetary Policy Review
July 8, Last: 2.25%, Westpac: 2.25%, Market: 2.50%
We expect the RBNZ to leave the OCR at 2.25% at its 8
July Review.  Much has changed over the past six weeks,
most notably oil prices have fallen much more rapidly than

expected. The related moderation in inflation pressure gives
the RBNZ more time to wait-and-watch how the economy is
tracking, especially with a large amount of important economic
data to be released after the July review. Forward guidance
should remain consistent with a lift in the OCR this year.
However, the message is expected to be more data dependent
than came across at the May Statement. While three MPC
members had voted for a rate hike in May, we think the “on

hold” decision may well be reached by consensus.

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX có xu hướng tăng trong tuần qua, +47.38 điểm tương ứng +2.6%, với thanh 
khoản giảm.Hàng xa xỉ & dệt may, hóa chất và bảo hiểm là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm 
rõ nét nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại có động thái bán ròng với giá trị 16 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở 
các mã FPT, CTG và TCB; trong khi tự doanh mua ròng nhẹ ở mức 92 tỷ đồng. 
Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng 983 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua nhưng 
đang duy trì vị thế mua lũy kế 2,029 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 19 hợp đồng nhưng 
đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế 2,111 hợp đồng. 
TTCK Việt Nam có tuần hồi phục thứ hai liên tiếp với diễn biến tích lũy giằng co, chỉ số VN-
Index tiến sát vùng kháng cự 1.900 điểm với thanh khoản suy yếu và nhà đầu tư nước 
ngoài duy trì đà bán ròng. Tâm lý thị trường tuần qua xoay quanh hai thông tin lớn: MSCI 
chưa đưa Việt Nam vào danh sách theo dõi nâng hạng trong kỳ đánh giá ngày 24/6, và 
việc Ngân hàng Nhà nước ban hành cơ chế loại trừ dư nợ 18 dự án hạ tầng trọng điểm của 
Vingroup, Sun Group và Masterise khỏi cách tính room tín dụng. Dòng tiền giao dịch thận 
trọng, chủ yếu mang tính thăm dò và phân hóa khi chỉ số tiến vào vùng kháng cự mạnh 
trong bối cảnh thiếu vắng nhóm dẫn dắt rõ ràng. Các yếu tố hỗ trợ như giá dầu hạ nhiệt 
sau khi thỏa thuận ngừng bắn Mỹ – Iran được duy trì, cùng tỷ giá USD/VND dịu lại, đã phần 
nào cải thiện tâm lý nhưng chưa đủ để kéo dòng tiền trở lại mạnh mẽ. Trong một, hai tuần 
tới, mùa công bố kết quả kinh doanh quý II với kỳ vọng khả quan được kỳ vọng sẽ trở 
thành chất xúc tác chính dẫn dắt xu hướng thị trường.

### Page 3

3

MSCI Accessibility Review 2026 
Chưa có điểm cộng, cần thêm thời gian thực hiện 
 Tóm tắt nội dung báo cáo của MSCI 
Rạng sáng 19/06/2026, MSCI đã công bố Báo cáo Đánh giá Khả năng Tiếp cận Thị trường 
2026 của 79 thị trường chứng khoán toàn cầu với tất cả 18 tiêu chí định tính. Đối với Việt 
Nam, 2 điểm đáng chú ý:

- MSCI ghi nhận thêm một số tiến triển trong quá trình cải cách thị trường như ghi 
nhận Việt Nam có thúc đẩy chương trình cải cách thị trường vốn, ứng dụng mô 
hình global broker, chuẩn bị go-live CCP, có tiến triển trong việc công bố thông 
tin bằng tiếng Anh, và một số định hướng nới lỏng room ngoại tại 1 số ngành.

- Dù vậy, toàn bộ 18 tiêu chí định tính đánh giá khả năng tiếp cận thị trường giữ 
nguyên so với kỳ đánh giá trước. Trong đó, 8 tiêu chí vẫn ở mức cần cải thiện, bao 
gồm: giới hạn sở hữu nước ngoài, room ngoại, quyền bình đẳng của nhà đầu tư 
nước ngoài, ngoại hối, công bố thông tin, thanh toán/bù trừ, cho vay chứng 
khoán và bán khống.

Quan điểm của KBSV  
Chúng tôi xin lưu ý rằng Việt Nam không nhất thiết phải đáp ứng được đủ 18 tiêu chí theo 
MSCI yêu cầu để được nâng hạng. Trên thực tế, phần lớn các thị trường mới nổi đều có 
trung bình từ 3-4 tiêu chí cần cải thiện (bị đánh dấu “-”), cá biệt nhiều thị trường đang 
được xếp hạng mới nổi còn nhiều tiêu chí cần cải thiện có Ấn Độ (7 tiêu chí), Trung Quốc 
A- Share (6 tiêu chí), Saudi Arabia và Brazil (cùng 5 tiêu chí) ...

Chúng tôi cho rằng TTCK Việt Nam có rất ít khả năng sẽ được nâng hạng ngay tại kỳ review 
tháng 6 (công bố vào 24/6/2026). Nguyên nhân là do Việt Nam bị giữ nguyên đánh giá từ 
tháng 6/2025 đến nay cho bộ 18 tiêu chí, bao gồm các tiêu chí trọng yếu mà hầu hết các 
thị trường mới nổi đều đã vượt qua như Clearing & Settlement (chờ đợi mô hình CCP hoàn 
thiện), FX liberalization, foreign room. Thay vào đó, khả năng Việt Nam được đưa vào 
nhóm cân nhắc nâng hạng nhiều khả năng sẽ rơi vào 2027, khi mà Chính phủ, Ủy ban 
Chứng khoán Nhà nước và các cơ quan liên quan đã đẩy nhanh nhiều cải cách trọng yếu, 
và được MSCI cũng như nhiều quỹ đầu tư nước ngoài công nhận.

Tác động đối với TTCK 
Áp lực chốt lời ngắn hạn có thể xuất hiện tại các nhóm cổ phiếu đã tăng theo kỳ vọng 
nâng hạng. Tuy nhiên, kết quả này không phủ nhận lộ trình nâng hạng trung hạn, do các 
cải cách trọng yếu vẫn đang được triển khai và một tổ chức khác là FTSE đã xác nhận 
nâng hạng Việt Nam từ tháng 9/2026.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây

### Page 4

4

Miễn room tín dụng cho 18 dự 
án trọng điểm

Cơ chế tín dụng đặc thù cho 18 dự án trọng điểm của Vingroup, Sun Group và Masterise 
— Ngày 22/6/2026, NHNN ban hành Công văn số 5386/NHNN-TD hướng dẫn các NHTM 
cấp tín dụng cho 18 dự án trọng điểm theo kiến nghị của Vingroup, Sun Group và 
Masterise. Theo đó, NHNN cho phép các NHTM loại trừ dư phần nợ cấp cho các dự án 
này khỏi hạn mức tăng trưởng tín dụng và có thể xem xét nới hạn mức tín dụng được 
cấp cho một hoặc nhóm khách hàng có liên quan (theo Quyết định 09/2024/QĐ-TTg). 
— Danh mục dự án được đề xuất là các dự án hạ tầng giao thông chiến lược trong lĩnh vực 
hàng không, đường sắt tốc độ cao, và các công trình phục vụ APEC. Tổng vốn huy động 
của nhóm dự án này là 752.1 nghìn tỷ đồng, thực hiện trong giai đoạn 2026-2033, trong 
đó khoảng 85% tổng vốn huy động dự kiến sẽ được giải ngân trong 3 năm đầu.  
— Với giả định tăng trưởng tín dụng toàn hệ thống năm 2026 đạt 15% (tương đương tổng 
dư nợ tín dụng đạt 21,384 nghìn tỷ đồng), quy mô vay vốn của nhóm dự án dự kiến sẽ 
chiếm khoảng 1% tổng dư nợ tín dụng cho mỗi năm trong giai đoạn 2026-2028, ước 
tính đóng góp vào tăng trưởng tín dụng thêm khoảng 0.5-1%/năm trong giai đoạn này.  
— Theo KBSV, quyết định này của NHNN phản ánh: 
(1) Cơ chế hỗ trợ tín dụng của NHNN đang mang tính chọn lọc khi mức đóng góp 
vào tăng trưởng tín dụng toàn hệ thống chỉ ở mức vừa phải, qua đó giúp khơi 
thông nguồn vốn cho các dự án trọng điểm mà không làm thay đổi đáng kể 
định hướng điều hành tín dụng chung của NHNN. 
(2) Tác động thực tế nhiều khả năng sẽ tập trung ở một số ngân hàng tham gia tài 
trợ dự án thay vì toàn hệ thống, tạo dư địa tăng trưởng tín dụng đáng kể nhưng 
đồng thời làm gia tăng mức độ tập trung tín dụng, yêu cầu các ngân hàng phải 
siết chặt khâu quản trị rủi ro.

Tác động lên mặt bằng lãi suất 
— Chúng tôi cho rằng quyết định này của NHNN sẽ tạo sức ép nhất định lên việc gia tăng 
vốn huy động của các NHTM tham gia giải ngân trực tiếp, đặc biệt khi chênh lệnh giữa 
huy động tiền gửi và dư nợ tín dụng toàn nền kinh tế tiếp tục nới rộng kể từ cuối 2025 
khiến tỷ lệ LDR thuần toàn hệ thống tăng mạnh (Biểu đồ 2).  
— Dù vậy, áp lực lên hệ thống sẽ được giảm bớt khi NHNN chủ động nâng tỷ lệ vốn ngắn 
hạn cho vay trung dài hạn từ 30% lên 40%, đồng thời cho phép tính thêm 20% tiền gửi 
KBNN vào tỷ lệ LDR (theo Thông tư 25/2026/TT-NHNN). Ngoài ra, chúng tôi cũng không 
loại trừ khả năng NHNN sẽ có thêm các chính sách hỗ trợ để bổ sung nguồn vốn cho 
vay các dự án này. Do đó, chúng tôi cho rằng mặt bằng lãi suất huy động sẽ ổn định ở 
vùng hiện tại, thậm chí có thể bắt đầu xu hướng giảm trong quý 3 nếu huy động của 
toàn hệ thống có sự cải thiện và giải ngân đầu tư công tăng mạnh.  
Xem đầy đủ tại đây

KQKD 1Q2026 tăng trưởng nhẹ

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ trợ 
sản lượng SCS từ 2H2026

Áp l ực t ừ Long Thành chưa đáng k ể 
trong ngắn hạn, tuy nhiên s ẽ dần gia 
tăng trong trung hạn

Cổ tức 2026 dự kiến ở mức 5,000 
VND/cổ phiếu

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục tiêu 
56,400 VND/cổ phiếu

1Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 272 tỷ VND (+2% yoy), hoàn thành 21% doanh thu 
kế hoạch 2026. Biên lợi nhuận gộp giảm xuống còn 78% (giảm 3 ppts so với 1Q2025) 
do tỷ trọng khách quốc tế giảm khi các chuyến bay Trung Đông bị cắt giảm mạnh từ 
tháng 2/2026. LNST đạt 218 tỷ VND (+2% yoy), hoàn thành 23% LNST kế hoạch 2026.

Mạng bay Trung Đông, đặc biệt Qatar Airways, đang phục hồi nhanh, giúp sản lượng 
hàng quốc tế của SCS c ải thiện từ 2H2026, bù đ ắp phần sản lượng sụt giảm trong 
1H2026, dự kiến đạt 224 nghìn tấn – đi ngang so với 2025.

Chúng tôi k ỳ vọng sau khi các h ạ tầng kết nối với sân bay Long Thành đ ược hoàn 
thiện, tỷ lệ phân bổ chuyến bay qu ốc tế giữa Long Thành và Tân S ơn Nhất sẽ dao 
động quanh kho ảng 75%/25%. Tỷ trọng phân b ổ hàng quốc tế tại TP . Hồ Chí Minh 
qua Long Thành 2H2027/2028/2029 dự kiến sẽ đạt 20%/35%/55%.

Chúng tôi cho rằng SCS sẽ tiếp tục duy trì tỷ lệ cổ tức tiền mặt cao trong các năm tới. 
Mức cổ tức năm 2026 dự kiến ở mức 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tỷ suất cổ tức 
8.8% so với mức giá đóng cửa ngày 19/06/2026.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đ ưa ra khuy ến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP với cổ phiếu SCS, mức giá mục tiêu 56,400 VND/cổ phiếu.

LNST quý 1-2026 tăng trưởng tương 
đối tích cực

Giá bán cá tra dự báo duy trì ở mức 
cao do nhu cầu sản phẩm thay thế 
các loài cá hoang dã

Lạm phát chi phí tăng, đư ợc bù đ ắp 
bởi khả năng tăng giá bán

Khuyến ngh ị: TRUNG L ẬP - Giá m ục 
tiêu 60,300 VND/cổ phiếu

VHC ghi nh ận doanh thu thu ần đạt 2,955 t ỷ VNĐ (+12% YoY) và l ợi nhuận sau thu ế 
của cổ đông công ty m ẹ đạt 266 t ỷ VNĐ (+38% YoY). Hầu hết các m ảng kinh doanh  
đều ghi nh ận tăng trư ởng cao , với doanh thu  phi lê cá tra đ ạt 1,546 t ỷ VNĐ (+14% 
YoY), doanh thu Collagen & Gelatin và Sa Giang đạt lần lượt 198 tỷ VNĐ (+21% YoY) và 
191 tỷ VNĐ (+16% YoY), nhờ nhu cầu phục hồi tại các thị trường trọng điểm.

Nguồn cung cá th ịt trắng biển hoang dã ti ếp tục bị hạn chế, khiến sản lượng nhập 
khẩu tiếp tục xu hướng suy giảm tại Mỹ và Châu Âu. Đề xuất gói trừng phạt thứ 21 của 
Liên minh Châu Âu (EU) hướng tới lệnh cấm hoàn toàn hải sản nguồn gốc Nga sẽ tạo 
cơ hội lớn cho các sản phẩm thay thế có chuỗi cung ứng ổn định như cá tra Việt Nam. 
Hiện khoảng 90% sản lượng cá Minh Thái nhập vào Châu Âu có nguồn gốc từ Nga và 
lách xuất sứ qua Trung Quốc.

Tác động từ cuộc chiến tại Iran đang đẩy chi phí vận chuyển và chi phí chung lên cao, 
gây áp l ực lên chu ỗi giá tr ị cá tra. Ngoài ra, chi phí cá gi ống và cá nguyên li ệu mua 
ngoài cũng liên tục lập đỉnh, tác động tiêu cực lên biên g ộp của doanh nghiệp. Mặt 
khác, biên lợi nhuận kì vọng được ổn định nhờ khả năng tăng giá từ Mỹ nhờ mức tồn 
kho thấp và nhu cầu tiếp tục ổn định.

KBSV dự phóng doanh thu và LNST của VHC đạt lần lượt 14,044 tỷ VNĐ (+17% YoY) và 
1,572 tỷ VNĐ (+15% YoY) trong 2026. Chúng tôi kì v ọng giá bán cao hơn s ẽ bù đắp 
được đà tăng giá của chi phí, cùng với sản lượng phục hồi tại thị trường Mỹ. 
Với định giá h ợp lý là 60,300 VNĐ/c ổ phiếu, tương đương ti ềm năng tăng giá là 5%, 
chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP đối với cổ phiếu VHC.

Quan điểm kỹ thuật 
Xu hướng & Hành động

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn 
(Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành một nến tăng điểm thân dài trên khung tuần, cho thấy tương quan 
cung cầu đang nghiêng về chiều hướng tích lũy. Trên khung ngày, đà tăng điểm chủ yếu 
bởi 2 phiên đầu tuần trước khi trải qua các phiên điều chỉnh do chịu áp lực của vùng kháng 
cự quanh 1890. Điểm đáng chú ý là khối lượng giao dịch duy trì ở mức khá thấp trong các 
phiên tăng, đi kèm sức lan tỏa hạn chế của dòng tiền. Diễn biến này cho thấy diễn biến của 
chỉ số đang có phần không phản ánh xu hướng của số đông các mã và hàm ý sự thiếu bền 
vững của nhịp hồi phục. Khi xu hướng tăng trung hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên thử thách lại vùng đỉnh cũ, nhưng áp lực rung lắc điều 
chỉnh nhiều khả năng còn tái diễn tại các vùng kháng cự phía trên. 
(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1950 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1900 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1830 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1780 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

May 2026: The inflation rise remains energy-led

Eurozone: Inflation remains driven by energy, pressures continue to develop but
without intensifying, confidence enjoys a respite
24/06/2026 3
We have selected two inflation measures (with and without energy) and six survey indicators: business confidence, as
measured by the PMIs in the manufacturing and services sectors; the “input prices” and “output prices" components of the
composite PMI (in order to identify direct inflationary pressures); the “suppliers’ delivery times” component of the

manufacturing PMI (a direct indicator of possible supply difficulties and supply-demand imbalance, and therefore, indirectly,
of inflationary pressures in the making); household confidence, as reflected in its “assessment of financial situation in the
next 12 months” component (in order to capture the impact of inflation on purchasing power). The trends in each of these
indicators are observed relative to month m=0, corresponding to the start of the conflict. Each line does not represent the

level of the indicator, but its cumulative variation compared to month m=0.
The assessment of the available data for May is rather positive. Granted, inflation keeps rising (+1.3 percentage points over
three months), but the contribution of the "energy" component remains dominant. According to the PMIs business climate
surveys, inflationary pressures continue to develop through rising input prices, while the increase in output prices is more
contained. However, the lengthening of delivery times continues, which is likely to fuel inflation. Business confidence in the

manufacturing sector has deteriorated a bit, after four consecutive months of improvement. This time, business confidence
in services and consumer confidence are sources of good news: the former has stopped falling and the latter has slightly
recovered. Now that the risk of a severe escalation of the conflict in Iran has decreased, it remains to be seen to which
extent survey data will improve starting in June.
Source: S&P Global, Eurostat, European Commission, Macrobond, BNP Paribas

## Document 315: The conflict in the Middle East_ a massive blow to growth in the Gulf (2026-07-15)

The conflict in the Middle East: a massive blow to growth in the Gulf
Stéphane Alby
CHART 1
Gulf countries: severe growth impact, but uneven
across economies

Pre-conflict Post-conflit
%                            Real GDP growth forecasts 2026
Saudi Arabia and Oman have been relatively unscathed, while the UAE, Kuwait and Qatar have been hit hard

Only two countries are not expected to experience a contraction in their GDP in 2026: Saudi Arabia and Oman. Having been largely spared
from the attacks and possessing infrastructure outside the Strait of Hormuz, Oman is a special case. Its economy is expected to grow by
around 3%, a rate close to the forecast at the start of the year. For Saudi Arabia, the downturn is larger. Initially forecast at 4.6%, growth is
now projected at 0.8% in 2026 due to the fall in hydrocarbon-related GDP . Nevertheless, the impact is less severe than for other countries in

the region thanks to the port of Yanbu on the Red Sea operating at full capacity. Excluding hydrocarbons, the strength of domestic demand is
also helping the Saudi economy to hold up better, unlike the UAE, whose outward-oriented economic model is vulnerable to the deteriorating
security situation. Despite the port of Fujairah, which is allowing trade to bypass the strait, a contraction in the UAE’s real GDP of close to 2%
is now anticipated, representing a shortfall of almost 7 percentage points compared with pre-conflict forecasts. For Kuwait and Qatar – the

least diversified economies in the Gulf, with no alternative to the Strait of Hormuz – the impact on growth is even more severe, as their real
GDP is expected to contract by 7 to 8%.
The macro-financial situation remains sound
Further revisions are possible, given the fragility of the negotiations between the United States and Iran. Nevertheless, the macro-financial
situation of the Gulf states is not giving cause for major concern. In addition to the considerable foreign assets managed by the region’s

sovereign wealth funds (over USD 4 trillion, or 175% of the GCC’s GDP), and government debt averaging just over 30% of GDP , relatively high
global energy prices could help to absorb some of the losses incurred during a complete closure of the Strait. At an aggregate level, the
GCC is therefore expected to record current account surpluses in 2026 (+2% of GDP , compared with +4.7% of GDP in 2025). Furthermore, the
budget deficit is expected to remain below 2% of GDP . The reorganisation of logistics flows in the region and the control of fuel prices have

also helped to contain the inflationary risk caused by the crisis. Although on the rise, average inflation in the Gulf countries is not expected
to exceed 2.5% this year, compared with 1.7% in 2025.

For the first time since 2009 (excluding COVID), the GDP of the Gulf Cooperation Council (GCC) is expected to contract this year (-0.8%),
whereas pre-conflict forecasts had predicted growth of 4.7%. Far from benefiting from the sharp rise in energy prices triggered by the
conflict, the scale of the downturn reflects the severity of a shock that is undermining many of the pillars of the Gulf economies. Never -

theless, the consequences are varying from one country to another, depending on the level of diversification and, above all, on the degree
of vulnerability to disruptions in the Strait of Hormuz.

## Document 319: The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem (2026-07-16)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem (2026-07-16).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-07-16
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank
Research
From Panda and Dim Sum Bonds to Deeper Capital Markets
The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem

### Page 2

Perry Kojodjojo | Chen Kan |  The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem |  July 20261
Key takeaways
– RMB’s global ambition. China is actively shifting RMB from one used for trade to a global investment and financing currency. The
Panda (CNY bonds issued by foreign entities within China)  and Dim Sum (CNH bonds issued in Hong Kong) bond markets are
the core pillars of this strategy, creating the necessary ecosystem for the RMB's international expansion.

– Panda bonds - Unlocking onshore China for global use. The onshore Panda bond market has matured into a strategic funding
tool for foreign issuers. Thanks to regulatory easing on fund usage, over half of the proceeds are now used offshore, making it a
flexible funding instrument and cementing the role of foreign participants who now account for over 30% of issuance.
– Dim Sum bonds - A deepening offshore hub. The offshore Dim Sum market is experiencing a surge in sophistication and

diversity, signaling its evolution from a policy-driven market into a mature offshore funding hub. It is now driven by new issuers
like tech firms, is offering longer-term bonds, and is set to receive a massive demand boost from new regulations allowing
mainland insurers to invest in the market.
– Next frontier - a focus on building a complete financial ecosystem. While both markets are growing, challenges remain, including
broadening the issuer base beyond financial institutions. The key to addressing these challenges is building a complete RMB

financing ecosystem, supported by deep secondary-market liquidity, reliable benchmark yield curves for price discovery, and
comprehensive risk-management tools. Ultimately, this is a gradual but foundational journey to establish the RMB as a more
widely funded, invested, and held international currency.
– Expanding European participation in RMB funding markets. While European entities dominate foreign issuance in both Panda
and Dim Sum markets, this participation is highly concentrated among a few financial sector repeat borrowers, with corporates

largely absent. Barriers include smaller deal sizes, a narrower investor base, and lack of market familiarity. As RMB
internationalization progresses, we anticipate broader international issuer participation, particularly from European corpora tes
given strengthening EU-China ties.

Perry Kojodjojo | Chen Kan |  The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem |  July 20262

### Page 4

Perry Kojodjojo | Chen Kan |  The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem |  July 20263
RMB Internationalization: A New Chapter Driven by Diverging Bond
Markets
China's policy stance on RMB internationalization has shifted from cautious to explicit , with the 15th Five-Year Plan (2026-30)
elevating it to a strategic priority. This shift is supported by a series of concrete actions in early 2026 amidst a global de-dollarization
trend. However, progress remains uneven. While there is strong official commitment, evidenced by record transaction volumes on

China's Cross-Border Interbank Payment System (CIPS) and a surge in RMB goods trade settlement to a projected 30% by 2025,
the currency's broader global footprint is still modest. The RMB holds only a 1-4% share across global payments, FX reserves, and
debt securities, which is disproportionately low compared to China's approximately 19% share of global GDP.
A fundamental change has occurred in the nature of cross-border RMB usage. The center of gravity has moved from trade to

investment flows, with capital and financial account transactions now constituting around 75% of total RMB settlements. This
means the next phase of internationalization requires building a deeper and more accessible financial ecosystem for raising a nd
deploying RMB capital globally. This includes creating more robust channels for funding, such as the onshore Panda and offshore
Dim Sum bond markets, alongside more sophisticated hedging tools and a broader array of attractive RMB-denominated assets.

Since 2025, these two key bond markets have entered a new, more dynamic chapter, accelerating on different tracks and
developing distinct characteristics. The onshore Panda bond market is experiencing a record year, with issuance in the first half of
2026 exceeding RMB160bn. This boom is driven by fundamental improvements to its architecture, including streamlined issuance
procedures and more flexible rules that allow issuers to remit proceeds overseas. This has transformed Panda bonds from a ring-

fenced currency pot into a source of globally usable RMB funding.
In contrast, the offshore Dim Sum market’s strong growth is driven by demand diversification. It is poised for a significant boost as
mainland insurers gain access via the Southbound Bond Connect, whose quota has been expanded to RMB 800bn, creating a new
source of demand for an increasingly foreign-led issuer base.
The markets are also evolving with unique "DNA." The Panda market's issuer base remains dominated by traditional sectors like

financials, while the Dim Sum market is diversifying and embracing tech. Issuance from IT giants has risen sharply in the Dim Sum
market as they seek cheap, long-tenor funding for their AI and cloud capex cycles. This reflects a difference in their duration profiles
as well. The Panda market remains overwhelmingly short-term, with nearly all issuance at three years or less due to bank-dominated
demand and a flat yield curve. Conversely, the Dim Sum market is showing clear maturity with a growing share of bonds in the 5-

year and longer tenors, which better aligns with the investment horizons of the tech sector.

The Panda bond market's record issuance year, even without a clear cost advantage, confirms its shift from a tactical choice to a
strategic funding tool. This maturation is driven by two key structural changes:
– From onshore tool to global funding. Regulatory easing has been a game-changer. Over half of Panda bond proceeds (53%) are

now used offshore, making it a flexible, globally-integrated RMB funding instrument.
– From niche to mainstay. Foreign issuers are now a cornerstone of the market, consistently accounting for over 30% of issuance
and showing early signs of diversification beyond just financial institutions.
While the market remains concentrated in short-term debt (<3 years) and dominated by domestic bank investors, the modest return
of foreign buyers is a positive signal for future market depth.

Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Bloomberg Finance LP, CEIC, Haver, Wind

Dim Sum market is becoming deeper and more diversified

Share of gross issuance (12MMA, %)
CN HK Foreign
The offshore Dim Sum market is undergoing a structural transformation, becoming deeper and more sophisticated. Three key

trends define its new trajectory.
– Issuer base revolution. The market is now driven by new participants. Foreign entities have become a cornerstone, accounting for
over 25% of issuance. Domestically, high-growth Tech/IT companies have decisively replaced real estate as the primary issuers,
reflecting growing demand for long-duration RMB funding to support AI, cloud and digital infrastructure investment.
– Market maturation. Issuance tenors are extending significantly. The growing share of bonds with 5+ year maturities, and the

emergence of a nascent 15Y+ market, signal rising investor confidence and more sophisticated funding strategies.
– Demand catalysts unlocked. Recent policy changes are a game-changer for demand. Allowing mainland insurers to buy Dim Sum
bonds via Southbound Bond Connect, alongside an increased quota (to RMB800bn) and wider product scope, is set to unlock a
massive and sustainable new pool of capital.
In short, the Dim Sum market is evolving from a niche offshore CNH pool into a more mature, diversified, and institutionally

supported market with a powerful new demand base on the horizon. Together, these developments are helping to establish the
foundations of a deeper and more liquid offshore RMB capital market.

### Page 11

Perry Kojodjojo | Chen Kan |  The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem |  July 202610
Building the offshore RMB ecosystem
Over the next five years, Panda and Dim Sum bonds are set to be the two core pillars driving the RMB’s evolution from a trade-
settlement currency into a global investment and financing currency . Together, these markets are creating a comprehensive
ecosystem that provides the essential assets for international players to fund, invest, and manage RMB holdings.

The Panda bond market, where foreign entities issue RMB-denominated debt inside mainland China, is successfully transitioning
from a policy initiative to a viable funding channel. Regulatory reforms have improved access for a diverse range of international
issuers, leading to growing market confidence and projected annual volume growth of 20-30%. However, significant hurdles remain,
including a narrow investor base, a lack of robust tools for hedging interest rate risk, and complex issuance rules that need further

standardization.
Concurrently, the Dim Sum bond market, consisting of RMB bonds issued outside mainland China (primarily in Hong Kong), is
solidifying its position as the primary offshore RMB hub. The focus here is on building a reliable benchmark yield curve through
regular government issuance and upgrading market infrastructure with enhancements to Bond Connect and innovations like digital
bonds. This market's growth, however, remains highly sensitive to offshore RMB liquidity, currency expectations, and overall

investor demand. Its long-term success depends on creating a deep and sustainable ecosystem rather than just growing issuance
volume.
Ultimately, the development of these bond markets is a gradual but foundational journey . Much like the "Samurai" market was for
the internationalization of the Japanese Yen, Panda and Dim Sum bonds are creating the necessary building blocks for the RMB's
global future. While this evolution is subject to broader factors such as capital account policies, market reforms, and the geopolitical

landscape, the strategic goal is clear: to establish a robust foundation for the RMB to be more widely funded, invested, and held
across the world.

### Page 13

Perry Kojodjojo | Chen Kan |  The Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem |  July 202612
EU issuers account for a large share of foreign RMB bond
issuance…
…but the issuances are overwhelmingly concentrated in financial
institutions
Expanding European participation in RMB funding markets
European entities are major players in the RMB bond market, representing over half of foreign Panda and a third of Dim Sum
issuance. However, the issuer base is highly concentrated, with financial institutions dominating (85-95%) and repeat borrowers

accounting for over 90% of activity. The key challenge lies in attracting new issuers, not retaining existing ones.
Primary hurdles include smaller deal sizes compared to major markets, a narrower investor base, and lack of market familiarity.
These factors often make the upfront investment in RMB funding infrastructure, documentation, and investor relationships
economically unfeasible.
Looking ahead, we anticipate these barriers will ease with RMB internationalization. A deeper offshore RMB ecosystem will support

larger benchmark issuance, broaden the investor base, and improve liquidity. Further development of CNH derivatives markets will
simplify currency risk hedging and RMB proceeds swaps. Additionally, China's low interest rates and growing China-related
business exposure could enhance RMB funding appeal for international corporates. As the cost-benefit equation improves, we
expect future market development to be driven by a broader range of international issuers, particularly European corporates, rather

than larger issuances from existing borrowers.

澳洲聯儲在 2026年至今已累計加息三次 ，主要因為國內
通膨壓力仍然高企 ，且勞動力市場持續偏緊 。澳洲近期
勞動力市場資料看似疲軟 ，但這很可能受到了季節性因
素的干擾 。五月 失業率為 4.36%，全職工作失業率 從
4.3%下滑到4.2%。其他勞動市場指標如就業不足率、失
業人口與工作招聘數量比值等均顯示勞動力市場仍然緊
俏。同時，5月消費支出強勁反彈，包括非必需消費類別
在內的支出均有所改善 ，支持我們對第二季實質家庭消
費成長 0.3% 的預測。
我們認爲澳幣後續仍有順風 ，金和銅大宗商品的貿易出
口順差應該會帶來財政順風 ，同時澳幣投機多頭已經明
顯下降 ，擁擠程度有所減少 。短期認爲澳幣應在 0.69-
0.71的目標價盤整 ，年底看至 0.73，更多上行空間應來

自於美元升息預期放緩 ，或更多在下半年 。我們認爲澳
幣的看法仍然正面 ，澳幣短期有所回撤 ，在0.70以下附
近或是加碼澳幣的良好點位 。除上述所述利好 ，澳洲央
行利率與美聯儲的利差轉正 ，避險成本大大降低甚至為
負，澳洲退休公積金的或加大對於海外頭寸的避險 ，對
澳幣也是利好因素。
《投資關鍵-澳洲勞動市場仍緊張，建議逢低留意配置》

資料來源：Haver Analytics,GS, 2026/6/26;中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/6/30
《投資關鍵-利率預期重定價下紐幣承壓，鷹派政策料
支撐短期表現》
市場延後美國降息時點的預期背景下，非美貨幣出現明
顯的重新訂價。在基本面對紐幣支撐有限，疊加近期美
元走強的影響下 ，NZD/USD進一步回落至 0.5650 的
水平。儘管油價迅速回落，降低紐西蘭的通膨壓力，但
若荷姆茲海峽恢復通行，各國可能啟動庫存回補，帶動
需求面支撐油價，使油價及其對通膨預期的影響維持波

動。
即使油價快速下跌並壓低通膨，紐西蘭央行基於以下原
因，在短期內仍很可能持續升息：1) 前瞻指引已明確轉
向鷹派；2) 利率仍遠低於中性利率，提供升息空間；3)
經濟成長與二輪通膨跡象仍支持預防性升息。預期 7/8
的央行議息結果將公布升息 1 碼，收窄的利差有望提振
紐幣短期表現。展望後市，預期第三季紐幣仍以區間震
盪為主。若中東局勢進一步明朗化且油價穩定於現價水
平，紐幣有望逐步轉強，朝 3Q26 的目標價 0.59 發展。
油價下跌的速度與幅度均遠超預期，但紐西蘭央行
仍偏向採取升息立場

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵-利差與油價雙重壓力未解 ，加幣偏弱格
局短期料延續》
延續上月跌勢，加幣自6月初約1.38回貶至1.42水準，
主要受美加利率預期分歧擴大影響 ，同時油價持續回
落，對淨能源出口國加拿大之基本面形成壓抑。
儘管近期領先指標略見改善 ，6月PMI回升、5月失業
率降至6.6%，但整體仍不足以扭轉成長偏弱格局，國
內的消費動能仍然沒有出現起色 。在成長低於潛在且
核心通膨趨緩的背景下，加拿大央行2026年政策立場
預計仍以觀望為主，利率缺乏上行的支撐，也缺乏進
一步升息動機。原先預期於4Q26與美國利差收斂，或
較難以實現，甚至存在進一步擴大的風險 。整體而言，
在基本面支撐有限和利差持續偏向美元的環境下 ，加
幣短期料維持偏弱的走勢。

，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要

標有進展之際，央行或有逐步升

• 評估人民幣隨穩中偏升，但央行

追漲，年底或往6.80附近靠攏。
《投資關鍵–人民幣穩中偏升，有助於人民幣國際化》
人民幣指數與美元指數在過去幾年一直維持良好的相關
性。直到2025年下半年開始，人民幣啟動中期獨立升值
行情。從圖中可見，儘管兩者的走勢形態仍高度相似，
但斜率已明顯拉開差距，顯示人民幣在升值方向的動能
顯著強於貶值方向。
近期CNH強勢的主要推動原因在出口商結匯潮，大陸銀
行代客結售匯金額(貨物貿易部分)於去年12月創新高達
1113億美元，且今年第一季亦維持相對高檔水準，出口
的強勢增長仍然使得結匯趨勢有所延續。而中國人行多
次強調保持匯率彈性、防範超調風險，今年以來發布的
人民幣中間價雖跟隨即期匯價調升，但多數時間均弱於
市場預估值，顯示人行的逆週期因數偏向緩和升勢引導。

人民幣在中東戰爭爆發以來，在國際貿易中的占比繼續
攀升至33%，在人民幣國際化的戰略目標有進展之際，
央行或有逐步升值之意。因此評估人民幣隨穩中偏升，
但央行不願過快升值，6.80的位置謹慎追漲，年底或往
6. 80附近靠攏。

《投資關鍵-通膨預期主導政策前景，升息預期為南
非幣提供短期支撐》
南非幣近期表現相對穩定 ，USD/ZAR匯價大致維持
於 16.45 附近 ， 市 場 焦 點 集 中 於 中 東 停 火 進 展 及
MOU架構下的後續談判，整體匯市呈現觀望態勢。
在宏觀環境方面 ，油價自高位快速回落 ，顯著紓緩
南非通膨壓力 。儘管短期仍存在反彈風險 ，整體不
確定性已較前期明顯下降 。與此同時 ，金價持續回
落對出口形成壓力 ，部分抵銷油價回落所帶來的正
面影響。
通膨數據方面 ，最新南非 CPI年增率上升至 4.5%，
惟核心通膨（3.8%）仍維持於央行目標區間內 。考
量南非央行已於5月採取預防性升息措施，加上油價
顯著回落 ，現階段通膨壓力較適合視為預期層面的
衝擊，而非單純價格層面的影響 。未來政策路徑將
更取決於通膨預期黏著性的評估 ，而非油價走勢本
身。市場普遍預期7月會議將升息一碼，進一步提升
的套息交易吸引力料會對南非幣帶來一定的支撐。
展望後市 ，在經濟成長動能偏弱 ，以及二輪通膨不

確定性仍存的環境下 ，南非幣趨勢性升值空間料將
受限。整體而言 ，匯率走勢仍將受地緣政治與油價
波動牽制，維持中性展望。

Thematic Report
重點提示
2026年下半年金融市場展望-
高利新常態下的經濟成長韌性
重點摘要與投資意涵：
✓高利率、高通膨環境延續，AI投資持續擴張成景氣核心動能
地緣政治風險與通膨黏著性使主要央行降息步伐趨於審慎 ，

消費動能受到高利率與高通膨制約；惟AI基礎設施、半導體
與雲端投資動能強勁，成為企業獲利與全球經濟最重要的成
長來源；配合財政政策支持，全球景氣與製造業維持正成長。
✓企業獲利改善支撐股市，但K型分化持續擴大
市場焦點可望由大型科技股逐步擴散至工業、金融、國防及
基礎建設等受惠產業。惟股市財富效果仍集中於高資產族群，
消費與資產表現分化現象短期難以改變。同時也開始重塑亞
洲國家的經濟與財政表現。
✓債務與財政風險成為市場重要變數
利率波動與信用風險事件發生頻率可能持續提高 。美國財政
赤字與債務供給持續增加，政府利息支出占GDP比重逐步接
近5%警戒區 。而日本推出 370兆日圓 《日本成長戰略 》計
畫，下半年政策引發債務疑慮，拖累股匯債市風險上升。
✓多元配置兼顧成長與防禦
全球景氣溫和成長，AI投資、製造業復甦及企業獲利改善支
撐下，下半年股市仍有機會維持震盪偏多格局。市場有望由
科技股獨強轉向輪動上漲，低基期及低評價資產具補漲機會。
債券提供收益價值，黃金與非美元資產則有助提升投資組合

韌性。在高波動環境下，透過分散配置可平衡收益與風險。

AI資本支出與地緣衝突成市場兩大軸線
《AI供應鏈與地緣衝突，股市呈現先抑後揚格局》

今年初美國在就業市場放緩疑慮以及聯準會重啟降息期待的多
空拉鋸下呈現盤整 ，然美國於2月28日發動「史詩怒火」行動
擊殺伊朗最高領袖哈米尼 ，隨後其子繼位展開反擊並封鎖荷姆
茲海峽，使國際油價飆漲，全球股市大幅崩落，僅能源、原物
料、公用事業類股受惠油價以及避險情緒表現較為亮眼。
4月後，隨著美伊走向談判未擴大為全面性戰爭 ，最壞情境預
期下降；同時，AI資本支出以及企業獲利預期持續上修，風險
情緒快速修復帶動股市收復跌幅外再創新高；其中以獲利明確
的AI硬體相關類股 (科技、通訊)及市場成為主要受惠者，又以
韓國、台灣與日本市場表現最佳。
相對股市的震盪，債市表現相對平淡，主要受到中東戰事起伏
主導。戰事初期，通膨預期大幅上升連帶澆熄市場對於聯準會
年內降息的期待，拖累債市在第一季度回落；而後隨戰事趨緩 ，
加上實際通膨數據雖走升但未失控 ，以及國際油價的回落 ，美
國10年期國債殖利率自高點小幅回落 ，債市雖出現小幅度反彈
但仍在高檔區間盤整，整體報酬貢獻仍以息收為主。
匯市部分，受到戰事帶來的避險需求以及對於聯準會貨幣政策

收斂的預期，美元表現優於非美貨幣 ，僅受惠商品大漲及升息
的商品貨幣(澳幣、南非幣)，以及受惠強出口貿易順差擴大帶
動結匯需求的人民幣表現較為強勢。

2026下半年高利高通膨與景氣擴張並行
《中東局勢趨緩，但高利高通膨仍將遞延一段時間》

回顧完上半年金融市場表現 ，展望下半年走勢首要定調今年的
整體經濟環境，而在兩大意外因素—中東戰事以及AI資本支出
超預期兩大主軸的交互作用下，本行預估下半年呈現高利高通
膨與經濟擴張並行經濟環境。
具體而言，6/15美伊簽署諒解備忘錄(MOU)以期在60天內達成
最終協議，消息一出國際油價大幅回落 ，但隨後雙方談判持續
反覆，國際油價亦呈現震盪 。且值得注意的是，本行評估，即
便海峽重新開放後，但受能源設備毀損修復與港口活動恢復常
態最少約需兩個月時間，加上急於回補庫存，配合夏季用油旺
季來臨，原油供需短缺的現象短期仍舊快速難解 ，而根據 EIA
預測(7/9)，第三季全球原油供需仍舊延續赤字格局。
此外，通膨具有滯後反應的特性 ，根據量化分析布蘭特原油價
格年增率對美國 CPI年增率具有顯著領先性 ，領先期數約為6–
9個月；因此 ，即便最終海峽運力回復 ，但預估下半年通膨壓
力仍難以快速緩解 ，IMF大幅上修2026年全球的通膨預測之外 ，
市場預估美國CPI在4Q26仍高達3.6%，短期難以回到3%以下。

因此，在通膨壓力無法快速消散下 ，主要市場貨幣政策調整朝
相對戰前收緊的方向避免通膨失控風險 。其中美國延後降息至
明年第一季，歐日澳則呈現升息趨勢；多數國家呈現不降息或
升息，今年以來利率走勢表現明顯高於年初預期。

高利高通膨與政策拉鋸，消費動能溫和

《高利高通膨制約消費擴張，所幸財政政策提供支撐》
在通膨使貨幣政策比預期緊縮下 ，預期今年下半年整體仍舊維
持在相對高利率與高通膨的經濟環境；而其最直接的影響 ，就
是對內需動能形成制約效果。
本行將實質利率、美國個人消費支出以及企業資本支出三者進
行標準分數(Z-Score)分析，即可發現實質利率對於個人消費支
出和企業資本支出兩者呈現相當顯著的負相關；統計結果顯示 ，
高利環境下需求面 (消費與投資)表現將受到制約 。同時，中東
戰事推升今年全球通膨預期 ，也使得主要國家消費者信心出現
普遍性的回落，同樣不利於消費動能的擴張 ，亦反映在今年消
費類股獲利及股價動能表現皆相對平淡。
所幸，受惠於財政政策支持 ，主要國家消費動能雖不強但仍可
維持溫和擴張。具體來說，川普政府在去年通過 「大而法案」
減稅措施，而根據 (Tax Foundation)資料顯示，美國今年1~4
月同期，美國總退稅金額較去年成長超過10%(超過3,500美元)，
對消費形成一定支撐；日本預計下半年減免食品消費稅 ，並推

出370兆元的《日本成長戰略》，皆提供需求面支撐力量。
綜上，整體終端需求雖在高利高通膨環境的制約下難以加速擴
張；但受惠財政政策以及穩健的就業市場支持，下半年整體消
費端可望延續溫和成長態勢。

AI資本支出動能超預期，支撐經濟持續擴張

《AI資本支出動能超預期，成為經濟成長主力》
消費動能受限高利高通膨壓抑 ，但驚喜的是投資動能在高利環
境下，不僅逆勢且呈現超預期的擴張，成為支撐今年美國經濟
成長的主要力量，也是上半年金融市場的主旋律之一。
具體而言，今年以來投資動能遠超預期的原因為 AI資本支出的
持續上修，AI基礎設施及相關硬體 、半導體晶片及雲端相關投
資不斷上修 。根據資料顯示 ，市場預估 2026年四大CSP業者
(亞 馬 遜、微軟 、 谷 歌 和Meta) 資 本 支 出，由 去 年 底 預 估 的
4,159億美元，今年以來一路上修 5月底預估的至 7,219億美元，
四大CSP業者資本支出年增率由13%升至73%，使得民間投資
成為支撐景氣的關鍵力量 。根據Bloomberg預估，今年美國平
均投資成長率達到 4.8%，遠高於過去 10年長期平均水準的
2.5%，且顯著優於今年民間消費平均成長率的 1.7%左右，消
費動能則低於過去10年平均成長率的2.7%。
具體而言，今年總經環境受到地緣戰爭影響 ，導致通膨與利率

未能如期回落，民間需求因此受到制約；而美國財政政策支持
消費動能尚可溫和擴張 ，但投資受惠於AI資本支出的超預期之
下，預期持續維持相對較快的成長動能 ，也讓美國經濟成長結
構延續去年第四季以來投資優於消費的樣態。
5

AI基建與應用雙引擎驅動，持續維持高成長
《AI從算力建設走向商業變現，成長動能持續擴散》

不過仍有不少投資人質疑 AI投資是否過度超前需求 ，資本支出
過熱的情況是否可能重演2000年網路泡沫。
然而，從企業採用率 、Token消耗量及雲端服務使用情況來看 ，
AI需求並未放緩，反而持續加速成長 ，顯示產業仍具備扎實的
基本面支撐。AI產業發展仍處於早期階段 ，成長動能正由基礎
建設投資逐步擴散至應用與商業化 。高盛預估全球Token消耗
量至2030年將較目前成長 24倍，反映企業導入 AI Agent、自
動化流程及決策輔助系統的需求快速增加。隨著模型成本下降、
推論效率提升及企業工作流程逐步整合 AI工具，AI產業有望從
高資本投入階段逐步邁向獲利改善階段。
從產業面觀察 ，AI 基礎 設 施 投 資仍 是 最 明確 的 成 長 主軸。
WSTS持續上修全球半導體市場展望 ，成長動能來自AI資料中
心、GPU、AI伺服器及 HBM等記憶體需求擴張 。另一方面 ，
OpenAI、Anthropic等LLM Labs營收也以遠高於傳統軟體產業
的速度成長，反映企業與個人對AI工具的付費意願持續提高 。

因此，現階段AI產業已開始出現商業化與獲利驗證成果 ，更接
近網際網路發展初期的基礎建設與應用同步成長階段 ，而非需
求不足所形成的泡沫。
6

消費溫和、投資拉動製造業與全球經濟維持擴張
《AI資本支出與政策支持全球景氣與製造業擴張》
2026年全球經濟預計維持溫和擴張，主要成長動能仍來自企業
投資與製造業回溫 。在AI基礎建設需求持續強勁帶動下 ，半導
體、資料中心及相關供應鏈資本支出保持高檔 ，支撐全球製造
業景氣表現。雖然地緣政治衝突一度造成能源價格與供應鏈波
動，但隨著市場逐漸適應相關風險 ，全球經濟並未出現明顯衰
退跡象，多數主要經濟體仍可維持正向成長。
然而，戰爭與通膨所帶來的影響 ，使製造業與服務業景氣復甦
呈現分化。製造業受惠於AI投資週期及企業提前拉貨需求 ，自
2025年以來表現相對強勁；相較之下，服務業則受到高利率、
高物價以及消費信心保守的影響 ，成長動能較為疲弱。展望下
半年，隨著通膨壓力逐步緩解及地緣風險趨於穩定 ，服務業景
氣有機會逐漸改善，而製造業則可能因提前拉貨效應減弱而回
歸較為溫和的成長軌道。
整體而言，全球經濟仍將呈現「消費溫和、投資支撐」的發展
格局。企業投資尤其是AI相關資本支出將持續扮演成長主力，

而民間消費則受到高利率環境及財富效果不均的影響，呈現相
對溫和的擴張態勢。在製造業與服務業景氣逐步重新平衡下 ，
2026年全球經濟有望延續成長，但產業、區域與消費族群之間
的差異仍將是未來經濟發展的重要特徵。
7

重點提示
￭
「
地緣政治不確定性
」
讓市場要求期限溢酬提
高
，

AI

共同塑造高利

。
「地緣不確定性+AI支出 」= 高利環境短期難解

《高利率環境短期難以明顯緩解》
2026年全球經濟雖可望維持溫和成長，但「地緣政治不確定性」
與「AI資本支出擴張」正共同塑造高利率環境長期化的新格局 。
儘管中東衝突風險較先前有所降溫 ，但能源價格波動對通膨仍
存在明顯落後效應，使主要央行在降息決策上保持謹慎 。同時，
供應鏈重組、國防支出增加及全球政治風險升溫 ，也使市場要
求更高的風險溢酬，推動長天期利率維持相對高檔 ，整體資金
成本難以快速下降。
另一方面，AI投資週期已進入大規模資本支出階段 。全球科技
企業持續投入資料中心 、半導體及電力基礎設施建設 ，不僅支
撐製造業景氣，也帶動資金需求上升 。從結構面來看，當企業
投資需求長期維持高檔 ，將進一步推升中性利率 （r*），使即
便進入降息循環，利率水準也未必能回到過去的超低利率區間 。
整體而言，地緣政治帶來的通膨黏著性 、AI投資推升的資金需
求，以及市場對風險要求更高的利率貼水 ，共同形成高利率環
境的重要支撐力量 。Bloomberg模型顯示，近年美債殖利率中
由風險情緒所貢獻的部分持續攀升 ，反映投資人要求更高報酬

以承擔不確定性風險。在此背景下，市場對快速降息的期待可
能持續落空，高利率環境短期內仍難明顯改變 。未來資產表現
將更依賴企業獲利成長與產業競爭力 ，而非單純受惠於資金成
本下降。

K型分化：財富效果擴大所得差距，消費續分化

《財富效果受惠的不對稱性分化資產增長速度》
2026年受到AI資本支出持續擴張，以及地緣政治衝突推升能源
與供應鏈成本的影響，全球經濟仍處於高利率 、高通膨的環境
之中，K型分化現象也在各個層面持續擴大。
AI投資熱潮帶動科技產業獲利與評價提升 ，推升全球股市尤其
科技類股大幅上漲。然而，股市帶來的財富效果具有顯著不對
稱性，由於金融資產主要集中於高所得與高資產族群，其財富
增長速度遠高於低所得家庭，使社會財富差距持續擴大。
若拉長觀察期間 ，美國標準普爾500指數自2023年起已連續三
年維持雙位數成長。但資本市場榮景並未平均分配至各階層 。
根據紐約聯邦儲備銀行資料 ，美國高所得家庭過去三年資產累
積增幅達 27%，低所得家庭則不到 5%。在資產持有規模與投
資報酬率差異下，高所得族群持續受惠於資產增值 ，而低所得
族群難以分享市場上漲紅利，財富差距因而進一步拉大。
財富效果的不對稱性也反映在消費結構兩極化 。一方面，高資
產族群受惠於資產增值 ，持續帶動精品、旅遊等非必需消費成
長；另一方面，低收入家庭受通膨侵蝕實質購買力 ，消費行為

趨於保守，並將支出集中於生活必需品 。隨著食品、住房等基
本開支持續上升，大眾消費市場成長趨緩 ，食品產業亦面臨成
長放緩壓力，僅高端與功能性產品仍維持相對穩健的需求。

K型分化：AI出口紅利財政豐收更抵禦波動

《擁科技出口紅利有望增添財政豐收更抵禦波動》
AI浪潮不僅改變消費結構 ，也開始重塑亞洲國家的經濟與財政
表現。隨著全球AI基礎建設需求持續擴張 ，具備半導體、伺服
器、電子零組件及高階製造能力的台灣 、韓國與越南，出口表
現明顯優於區域其他國家 。即使在全球高利率及貿易環境不確
定性下，AI相關產品出口仍維持高速成長 ，使科技受惠國的經
濟成長動能與外部收支表現持續領先亞洲。
更重要的是，AI出口所創造的企業獲利與稅收紅利 ，正逐步反
映在政府財政改善上。台灣、韓國、及越南近年受惠科技產業
景氣與出口成長，財政收入增幅普遍優於其他亞洲經濟體 ，政
府因此擁有更大的政策調節空間 。當面臨能源價格波動 、通膨
壓力或外部需求放緩時 ，政府可透過補貼、減稅或公共投資等
措施平滑景氣循環，降低經濟受到外部衝擊的影響 。例如台灣
2025年全民普發1萬元，韓國2026年發放燃料救濟金60萬韓元。
因此，亞洲過去相對依賴房地產、原物料或傳統製造業成長的
模式，AI已逐步成為部分亞洲國家的新興經濟護城河。科技出

口不僅帶動企業獲利與就業成長，也同步強化財政體質與政策
應變能力。在全球景氣與地緣政治波動加劇的環境下 ，具備AI
供應鏈優勢的經濟體，將更有能力維持成長韌性 ，並成為未來
幾年亞洲表現相對突出的受惠區域。

變數1：聯準會新任主席政策框架調整引波動
《聯準會新任主席計畫引入截然不同的框架，市場波

動可能放大》
接下來不免俗要對市場風險進行提醒 。首先，隨著新任聯準會
主席華許(Warsh)正式上任 ，市場除了關注經濟與通膨走勢外 ，
也應開始重新評估美國貨幣政策框架可能變化帶來的風險。
過去二十年市場已習慣聯準會透過前瞻指引 、經濟預測及充分
溝通引導市場預期，因此即使面臨金融危機或通膨衝擊 ，市場
仍能大致掌握政策方向 。然而，新任主席華許則主張降低前瞻
指引依賴、減少市場溝通，並強調以制度改革及長期通膨管理
為核心，意味未來貨幣政策可預測性可能低於過去。
從政策立場觀察，華許對量化寬鬆及聯準會資產負債表擴張向
來抱持較為保守態度 ，同時認為AI帶來的生產力提升可能支撐
經濟成長，因此對降息與流動性寬鬆的態度未必如市場預期積
極。在美國財政赤字及國債供給持續增加的背景下 ，若聯準會
逐步降低對市場的隱性支持 ，高利率環境可能維持更長時間 ，
也將使利率、匯率及風險資產價格對政策訊號更加敏感 。不過
從華許的發言判斷，縮表應該至少要到2027年才會實行，下半
年將先維持充裕準備金制度。

而根據歷史經驗回測顯示，聯準會主席更替初期往往伴隨市場
重新評價政策框架的過程。過去多數新任主席上任後三個月內 ，
股市平均回調幅度 -12%，任一時間點三個月內平均回調幅度 -
6.7%明顯放大，因此，短線應留意來自政策預期調整所引發的
波動加劇。

變數２：債務疑慮引發的市場震盪頻繁

《債務與利率風險持續反覆影響金融市場》
綜上所述，中東地緣政治反覆帶來的通膨壓力、AI投資推升的
資金需求，以及市場對風險要求更高的利率貼水，共同支撐高
利率環境延續。然而，高利率維持時間愈長，其影響將不再侷
限於經濟層面，而是逐漸擴散至政府財政與債務可持續性議題 ，
成為金融市場新一輪波動的重要來源 。回顧新冠疫情以來，各
國為因應疫情衝擊大幅擴張財政支出 ，使全球政府債務規模明
顯攀升。在零利率時代，龐大債務負擔尚可透過低融資成本維
持運作，但隨著利率快速走升，利息支出已成為各國財政的重
要壓力來源。近年市場已多次出現 「信用疑慮升溫、殖利率上
升、金融條件緊縮、資產價格修正」的循環，包括2022年英國
國債危機 、2023年SVB倒閉事件 ，以及2024年日本升息引發
全球套利交易平倉等，皆顯示債務問題已從潛在風險逐步演變
為市場波動的重要推手 。根據預測資料顯示，主要國家利息支
出占GDP比重未來幾年仍將持續攀升 ，其中美國預計至 2027
年前後將接近IMF定義的5%高風險警戒線。隨著各國政府面臨

更高的再融資壓力與財政負擔 ，在通膨與高利率短期難以明顯
緩解的背景下，市場對債務可持續性的關注勢必持續升溫 。換
言之，債務風險已不再只是單一事件，而更可能成為未來金融
市場的「慢性壓力來源」，使股債市場波動頻率與風險溢酬中
樞較過去幾年進一步提高。

債務引發的市場震盪頻繁，留意美日政策風險
《下半年留意美日政策風險》
展望未來一年，在高利率環境延續與政府債務持續攀升下 ，又
以美國與日本債務可能成為全球金融市場最重要的波動來源。
具體來說，2026年美國同時面臨「支出增加」與「收入減少」
的雙重壓力，一方面地緣政治升溫推高國防支出與利息負擔 ，
另一方面去年大而美法案的減稅政策延續使政府稅收減少 ，同
時IEEPA課徵關稅判決違法也面臨退稅壓力 ，2026年美國財政
赤字較去年預 估大增 3000億美元以上 。因此 ，根據 BPC與
CBO估計，美國最快可能於 2027年上半年再度接近債務上限 ，
使債務協商與財政爭議再度成為市場關注焦點。
日本部分 ，(6/24)日本首相高市早苗公佈 《日本成長戰略 》，
政府預計2040財年前投入370兆日元重點支持17個戰略產業，
(AI和半導體領域投資規模達 101.6兆)。官方預估增長戰略將使
日本全要素生產力以及潛在經濟成長率將大幅提高 ，稅基也會
大幅增加，日本債務占GDP比重有望下降。但市場更擔心的是

日本財政紀律以及債務負擔可持續性 ，該計畫預計每年增加近
25兆日圓支出，加以下半年財政框架的進一步放寬 ，以及預期
國家軍隊正常化修憲後 ，國防支出的大幅提高 ，導致市場對日
本債務可持續的擔憂再度升高，拖累日本近期股債匯市三殺。
綜上，短期間不論是美國或日本皆可能因為信用事件或財政壓
力觸發債務疑慮，導致利率短線大幅走高而拖累股市震盪。所
幸，兩國經濟基本面溫和擴張，還不致因此引爆系統性危機。
13

高利高通膨壓抑估值，政策與獲利支持正向發展
《高利環境延續，政策與獲利支持景氣與股市擴張》

2026年下半年金融市場處於景氣溫和擴張與高利、高通膨並存
環境，市場波動仍高；然 ，AI基礎設施、半導體及雲端相關投
資具高度確定性，持續提供景氣支撐，加以主要國家財政政策
支持，下半年經濟基本面仍具韌性，全球景氣延續溫和擴張格
局不變。建議透過多元配置與動態調整策略，兼顧防禦與成長。
股市方面，高利率與較高風險溢酬仍將限制評價面擴張 ，但全
球景氣維持溫和成長 ，在AI投資、製造業復甦及企業獲利改善
支撐下，下半年股市仍有機會維持震盪偏多格局 。隨獲利成長
逐步擴散至工業、金融、國防及基礎建設等產業 ，市場有望由
科技股獨強轉向輪動上漲，低基期及低評價資產具補漲機會。
債券方面，市場對聯準會降息時點的預期持續遞延 ，加上美國
財政赤字、債務供給增加及政策不確定性影響 ，美國10年期公
債殖利率預估仍將維持 4.2%至4.6%區間震盪 。雖然長天期公
債資本利得空間有限，但目前殖利率已具配置吸引力 。信用債
則受惠於景氣持穩與企業違約風險偏低 ，投資等級債及非投資
等級債仍具收益優勢。

匯市方面，隨美國通膨降溫及降息循環逐步展開 ，美元強勢高
峰可能已過，下半年預期維持偏弱整理 。日本財政擴張、歐洲
政策轉趨穩健，以及部分新興市場降息帶動資金回流 ，均有助
非美元貨幣表現。亞洲貨幣與部分新興市場貨幣可望優於上半
年，黃金則持續受惠於降息預期 、地緣政治風險及央行增持需
求，維持中長期偏多趨勢。

## Document 389: インドネシアに新たな｢格下げリスク｣ ～S&Pダウ･ジョーンズ･インデックスも格下げを警告､市場の信認低下が進む可能性も～

Source PDF: all report pdf/インドネシアに新たな｢格下げリスク｣ ～S&Pダウ･ジョーンズ･インデックスも格下げを警告､市場の信認低下が進む可能性も～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-07-08
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 7 月 8 日

1/3 
インドネシアに新たな｢格下げリスク｣ 
～S&P ダウ･ジョーンズ･インデックスも格下げを警告､市場の信認低下が進む可能性も～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 2026 年のインドネシア金融市場では、株式、通貨ルピア、国債のすべてに売り圧力がかかっ
ている。きっかけは、指数算出会社の MSCI が同国株式市場の情報開示や透明性に懸念を示
し、 指数採用の停止や格下げの可能性を警告したことにある。FTSE ラッセルも指数見直しを
延期し、さらにＳ＆Ｐダウ・ジョーンズ・インデックスも「新興国」から「フロンティア」
への格下げ可能性を示すなど、主要指数算出会社による警戒感が相次いでいる。 
○ インドネシア当局は、 最低浮動株比率の引き上げなど市場改革を打ち出したものの、MSCI は、
価格形成を歪める取引の兆候や英語による情報開示の不十分さなど、なお多くの課題が残る
と指摘し、審査を 11 月まで再延長した。すでに一部企業はMSCI 関連指数から除外されてお
り、特定株主への所有集中が問題視されている。仮に格下げが実施されれば、パッシブ投資
家を中心に資金流出が加速し、株式相場への下押し圧力が強まる可能性がある。 
○ また、インドネシア政府は 2026 年の財政赤字がＧＤＰ比▲2.85％と当初予想を上回るとの
見通しを示している。原油高による補助金歳出の増加が財政悪化の要因となっており、今後
は格下げ懸念と財政運営への不安が長期金利の上昇を招くリスクがある。 さらに、 ナディム ・
マカリム元教育相への有罪判決を機に、恣意的な法運用への懸念も高まっており、市場の信
認が一段と低下する可能性に引き続き注意が必要と考えられる。

2026 年のインドネシア市場では、主要株価指数（ジャカルタ総合指数） 、通貨ルピア、国債のす
べてに売り圧力がかかっている（図１、２）。きっかけは、指数算出会社のMSCI が１月末に同国株
式市場の情報開示に懸念を表明したことにある。具体的には、 世界株指数などへの同国株の新規採
用を停止し、 投資可能株式数の上限も据え置いたうえで、 ５月までに透明性向上への取り組みが進
まなければ、同国株の比率引き下げやフロンティア市場への格下げを示唆した。さらに、直後に指

### Page 2

2/3 
数算出会社のFTSE ラッセルも、 定例で実
施予定であった同国株指数の見直し延期
を発表した。 
その後、当局は最低浮動株比率の大幅
引き上げなど、海外投資家の懸念解消に
向けた対策を矢継ぎ早に打ち出し 、市場
改革を加速させる姿勢を示した。このた
め、MSCI は５月に当局の改革姿勢に一定
の評価を示したものの、審査を６月まで
延長した。しかし、６社を「MSCI インド
ネシア指数」から除外し、うち１社を
「MSCI インドネシア小型株指数」 に移管
した。加えて、13 社を MSCI インドネシ
ア小型株指数から除外した。 これにより、
計 18 社が同社算出指数から除外された。
除外対象となった企業は、特定株主への
所有集中という共通した特徴がみられ
る。 
MSCI は６月 22 日、同国株に対する審
査を 11 月まで再延長すると明らかにし
た（注１）。見直し時点までに当局の改革
に十分な進展が見られない場合、格下げ
に関する協議などさまざまな選択肢を検討するとした。価格形成を歪める協調的な取引の兆候や、
英語による詳細な市場情報の提供が不十分であることを指摘し、 取り組むべき課題が依然多いこと
を示唆した。なお、当局がこれまでに発表した一連の改革を評価する一方、市場全体に一貫した持
続的な取り組みが必要であると指摘し、事実上の「条件付き据え置き」に近いと考えられる。仮に
フロンティア市場への格下げが実施されれば、 パッシブ投資家を中心とする資金流出が加速し、 結
果的に相場のさらなる下押し圧力となることが懸念される。 したがって、 当面は神経質な値動きが
続く可能性が高い。 
指数算出会社であるＳ＆Ｐダウ・ジョーンズ・インデックスは７月７日、株式市場の透明性に関
する問題を理由に、 「新興国」から「フロンティア」に格下げを行う可能性を警告した。具体的に
は、インドネシアを 2027 年の市場区分見直しに際して対象となる可能性のある「監視リスト」に
加えた。同社は、当局による情報開示に関する懸念や市場の流動性への潜在的な影響、保有構造の

注１ ６月24 日付レポート「MSCI､インドネシア株式市場の審査を再延長､格下げリスクは残る」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/623408.html） 
図 2 長期金利(10 年債利回り)の推移

（出所）LSEG より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成 
図 1 ルピア相場(対ドル)と主要株価指数の推移

（出所）LSEG より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
透明性向上への取り組みを注視するとしたうえで、 状況が悪化した場合には同国の証券に対する特
別措置の導入を検討する必要があると説明した。特別措置の導入から１年経過しても、 問題が解決
しない場合には、市場区分の見直しを実施するとした。この内容は、前述したMSCI や FTSE ラッセ
ルによる警告と類似していると捉えられる。 
こうしたなか、同国政府は 2026 年の財政赤字がＧＤＰ比▲2.85％と当初予想（同▲2.68％）を
上回り、法定上限（３％）に近付くとの見通しを示している。その理由について、プルバヤ財務相
は、 商品市況の上昇が歳入を押し上げる一方、原油高による補助金歳出の増大を受けて歳出が歳入
を上回るペースで拡大することを理由に挙げている。そのうえで、効率化の一環として 40 兆ルピ
ア規模の歳出を追加削減することが可能との見方を示した。 一方、 国会予算委員会はその倍 （80 兆
ルピア） 規模の歳出削減を要求しているとされる。中東情勢の緊迫化を受けた原油高の一巡を受け
て、 長期金利は下落するなど落ち着きを取り戻す動きをみせた （図２） 。 しかし、 先行きは格下げ懸
念の高まりに加え、財政運営への懸念が金利上昇を招く可能性もある。金融市場では、ジョコ前政
権で教育・文化・研究・技術相を務めたナディム・マカリム氏が有罪判決を受けたことを機に、恣
意的な法運用への懸念が高まっているが（注２） 、市場への信認が一段と低下するリスクにも注意
が必要である。
